Method: 方法分三层：(1) 范畴签名聚类：每篇论文p被建模为一个小范畴C_p，其对象为抽取出的类型化研究实体，态射为论文断言的关系；先按范畴签名对论文聚类以缩小候选范围。(2) 函子保持门控：跨论文桥接定义为部分函子候选F: C_p→C_q，要求保持对象种类与被覆盖的关系类别；通过复合与恒等箭头检测类比是否保持关系链，以约17:1比率过滤不可靠的跨领域候选。(3) 六轴LLM合理性评判：对通过门控的候选从六个轴进行LLM可解释性评估。被过滤的候选均保留逐轴理由，使门控充当日志层而非静默过滤器。

Result: 在数万篇全文本解析论文语料上，四组消融条件下：范畴门控以约17:1的比率过滤跨领域候选；被接受想法的定量可证伪率始终保持在83%以上；每个被拒绝的候选都附带逐轴理由被保留，因此门控同时发挥日志与审计作用。结果表明添加范畴结构显著提升了构想的可靠性与可追溯性。

Conclusion: 作者的核心主张是：将论文从扁平对象提升为带复合与恒等结构的小范畴，并以部分函子作为跨论文桥接的数学判据，能够恢复被现有方法商掉的关系链推理，显著提高自动研究构想的质量（高可证伪率）与透明性（逐轴日志）。该框架为LLM构想系统提供了一条可组合、可验证的替代路径。

Abstract: Automated research-idea generation systems built on large language models (LLMs) share a structural weakness: they reduce ideation to free-text recombination, random paper pairing, or embedding-similarity retrieval. The three approaches fail in the same way: each treats a paper as a flat object, a string or a vector, and so quotients away the typed problem-method-metric-claim arrows a researcher actually uses when reasoning about a cross-domain analogy. We recover the missing structure with the minimal piece of category theory that a typed graph alone does not provide: composition, together with identity arrows, which makes it possible to ask whether a proposed analogy preserves relation chains. Concretely, each paper $p$ is modelled as a small category $C_p$ whose objects are extracted typed research entities and whose morphisms are the relations the paper asserts; a cross-paper bridge from $p$ to $q$ is then a partial functor candidate $F: C_p -> C_q$ that preserves object kinds and covered relation classes. We instantiate the model as a three-layer algorithm: categorical signature clustering, a functor-preservation gate, and a six-axis LLM plausibility judge. Evaluated on a corpus of tens of thousands of full-text-parsed papers under four ablation conditions, the categorical gate filters cross-domain candidates at roughly a 17:1 ratio while the quantitative-falsifier rate of accepted ideas stays above 83% throughout; every rejected candidate is retained with its per-axis rationale, so the gate doubles as a logging layer rather than a silent filter.

</details>


### [96] [Multilingual Verifier Bias in RLVR: Benchmark, Rollout Diagnosis, and the Cross-Lingual Selection Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20362)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在数学推理的多语言强化学习（RLVR）场景中，精确匹配（exact-match）答案验证器会把格式与书写系统差异转化为依赖语言的假阴性奖励噪声，从而破坏“验证器是语言中立的奖励函数”这一假设。作者提出了一个可复用的多语言 RLVR 奖励审计协议，并通过系统实验证明：按语言和答案接口审计奖励，应被置于优化之前。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 是训练大语言模型进行数学推理的标准方法，其核心假设是答案验证器可作为语言中立的奖励函数。然而在多语言环境下，该假设失效：精确匹配验证器对格式（如数字形式）和书写系统（如日文、中文与英文的数字表达差异）敏感，会以不同比率拒绝“可信正确”的答案，形成依赖语言的假阴性奖励噪声。这种系统性偏差尚未被充分认识，论文旨在揭示并量化该问题。

Method: 作者提出一套可复用的多语言 RLVR 奖励审计协议，包含三个组件：(1) 验证器鲁棒性测试套件（verifier-robustness suite）；(2) 滚动输出诊断流程（rollout-diagnosis）；(3) 语言条件化奖励误差指标（language-conditioned reward-error metrics）。实验设置包括：在 MGSM 数据集上对 Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 进行 k=8 采样；使用纯数字探针（plain-numeric probe）将机制定位到最终答案接口；引入无需可信标签的目标本地聚合规则（target-local aggregation rule）以评估跨语言选取瓶颈；并在 MGSM250 与 483 题的 MATH-500 子集上复现；最后通过规则化的 GRPO（rule-GRPO）训练审计验证。

Result: 在 MGSM 滚动输出（k=8）上，精确匹配代理按语言以显著不同的比率拒绝可信正确答案：Qwen3-8B 的假阴性率在日文（JP）达到 0.642，而英文（EN）为 0.122、中文（CN）为 0.073。纯数字探针将机制定位到最终答案接口：接口模型可使奖励误差的 VLB（变分下界）降为零，但残余准确率差距不变，说明问题源于答案接口而非推理能力。跨语言选取瓶颈方面：目标本地聚合规则在无可信标签下可缩小 55–78% 的平均选取差距，且超过 95% 的修复需要真正的跨语言信息支持；该瓶颈在 MATH-500 上同样复现。受控训练审计表明，rule-GRPO 提升了可信准确率，但奖励误差 VLB 仍保持高位。

Conclusion: 核心结论是操作性的：多语言 RLVR 奖励在优化之前，必须按语言和答案接口分别进行审计。精确匹配验证器引入的语言依赖假阴性噪声会误导训练信号，且跨语言选取瓶颈意味着单纯依靠单一语言的本地信息无法充分修复偏差；规则化 GRPO 虽能提升准确率，但无法消除奖励误差。因此，构建多语言 RLVR 流水线时应将奖励审计视为必要前置步骤。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a standard recipe for training large language models on mathematical reasoning, where an answer verifier serves as a language-neutral reward function. We show that this assumption fails in multilingual settings: an exact-match verifier turns format and script variation into language-dependent false-negative reward noise. We introduce a reusable protocol for auditing multilingual RLVR rewards: a verifier-robustness suite, a rollout-diagnosis procedure, and language-conditioned reward-error metrics for Japanese, English, and Chinese answers. On MGSM rollouts with k=8, the exact-match proxy rejects trusted-correct answers at sharply different rates by language across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct; for Qwen3-8B, the false-negative rate reaches 0.642 on JP against 0.122 on EN and 0.073 on CN. A plain-numeric probe localizes the mechanism to the final-answer interface: an interface model drives reward-error VLB to zero while the residual accuracy gap is unchanged. We then expose a cross-lingual selection bottleneck: on MGSM250 rollouts, a target-local aggregation rule using no trusted labels closes 55-78% of the average selection gap, and over 95% of repairs require genuine cross-lingual support. The bottleneck replicates on a 483-problem MATH-500 set. A controlled training audit shows that rule-GRPO raises trusted accuracy while the reward-error VLB stays high. The unifying message is operational: multilingual RLVR rewards should be audited by language and by answer interface before they are optimized.

</details>


### [97] [Hadith computational science in the age of large language models: a critical narrative review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20364)
*Md. Ashraful Haque,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性叙事综述，评估了transformer模型、检索增强流水线和大型语言模型（LLM）如何重塑圣训（hadith）计算科学。综述在批评既有文献的基础上，对代表性原创研究进行逐篇评估，并综合伊斯兰学者与领域专家的观点，指出该领域进展不均：数据资源、分割任务、叙述者与来源验证问题及LLM辅助工作流有所成熟，但仍受限于狭窄语料库、基准可比性弱、合成到真实迁移缺口、叙述者身份消解、预处理脆弱、可复现性差及专家验证不足等问题，并据此提出将圣训计算定位为证据基础设施问题、以知识整合、溯源和专家监督为核心的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于圣训计算科学的综述虽记录了文献的增长，但尚未提供批判性视角来说明哪些进展在方法论上稳健、哪些仍受制于基准、哪些未解决问题仍限制学者使用。作者旨在填补这一空白，给出一个兼具批判性、逐篇评估与专家视角整合的综述。

Method: 采用批判性叙事综述（critical narrative review）方法，包含三个层面：对既有综述的批判、对代表性原创研究的逐篇评估（paper-level appraisal）、以及对伊斯兰学者和领域专家关于真实性、权威性与负责任使用观点的综合。

Result: 发现进展不均衡：数据资源扩展、分割任务成熟、叙述者与来源验证问题得到更好形式化，LLM辅助工作流支持语料库规模增强、多语言访问和基于grounded evaluation的评估；但同时仍受制于狭窄语料库、弱基准可比性、合成到真实迁移缺口、叙述者身份消解、预处理脆弱性、可复现性有限以及缺乏专家grounded验证。此外，关键缺口存在于基准之外：非正典与冷门语料、注疏与解释性文献、与《古兰经》和seerah的跨源链接、以及面向fiqh（法学）的证据支持。

Conclusion: 圣训计算不应仅被评估为孤立的模型性能问题，而应被视为证据基础设施（evidence infrastructure）问题，需依赖知识整合、溯源（provenance）和专家监督。基于此，作者定义了使该领域方法论更强且对伊斯兰学术更有用的研究议程。

Abstract: We examine how hadith computational science is being reshaped by transformer models, retrieval-grounded pipelines, and large language models (LLMs). Recent reviews document growth in the literature, but they do not yet provide a critical account of which advances are methodologically robust, which remain benchmark-bound, and which unresolved problems still limit scholarly use. We address this gap through a critical narrative review that combines critique of existing reviews, paper-level appraisal of representative original studies, and synthesis of Islamic scholar and domain-expert perspectives on authenticity, authority, and responsible use. We find uneven progress. Data resources have expanded, segmentation tasks have matured, narrator and source-verification problems are better formalized, and LLM-assisted workflows now support corpus-scale enrichment, multilingual access, and grounded evaluation. At the same time, progress remains constrained by narrow corpora, weak benchmark comparability, synthetic-to-real transfer gaps, narrator identity resolution, preprocessing fragility, limited reproducibility, and sparse expert-grounded validation. We show that important gaps lie beyond dominant benchmarks: non-canonical and obscure corpora, commentary and explanatory literature, cross-source links with Qur'an and seerah, and fiqh-facing evidence support. We argue that hadith computation should be assessed less as isolated model performance than as an evidence infrastructure problem requiring knowledge integration, provenance, and expert supervision. On this basis, we define a research agenda for making the field methodologically stronger and more useful to Islamic scholarship.

</details>


### [98] [Trilingual Topic Modeling of Sri Lankan Parliamentary Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20365)
*Himath Dhanapala,Haren Daishika,Himandhi Kuruppu,Sithija Seneviratne,Ashini Kavindya,Patalee Narasinghe,Sandeepa Weerasekara,Nisansa de Silva,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对斯里兰卡议会辩论（Hansard）三语语料库（僧伽罗语、泰米尔语、英语）提出一个端到端框架：先用LLM从排版复杂的PDF中抽取文本，再用多语言嵌入与基于密度的聚类进行无监督主题建模；并扩展了混合语义-词汇方法BiTopic以提升可解释性并回收被误判为噪声的演讲。在2017–2026年间19,553篇演讲上恢复出30个宏观主题，聚类纯度BCP达0.673，时间轨迹与2019年复活节袭击和2022年经济危机等重大国家事件吻合；传统LDA因跨语言碎片化而失效，本方法跨三种语言成功识别主题结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡议会辩论语料是三语（僧伽罗语、泰米尔语、英语）且含语码混合内容，但由于排版复杂的PDF、多语言文字体系和黏着语形态，现有标准NLP流程无法处理，因而该语料一直不可用。

Method: 提出端到端框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 基于LLM的文本抽取，解决复杂PDF布局与多语言文字问题；(2) 采用多语言嵌入与基于密度的聚类（如HDBSCAN风格）进行无监督主题建模。此外提出混合语义-词汇扩展方法BiTopic，以提升主题可解释性并找回在嵌入空间中被视为噪声而被丢弃的演讲。

Result: 在19,553篇演讲（2017–2026）上恢复出30个宏观主题，聚类纯度BCP为0.673；主题的时间轨迹在无监督情形下与重大国家事件（2019年复活节恐袭、2022年经济危机）对齐。传统LDA因跨语言碎片化而失效，而所提方法无需监督即可在三语间识别主题结构。

Conclusion: 该框架证明多语言嵌入+密度聚类、并结合语义-词汇增强，能在低资源、三语、语码混合的议会语料上实现有效的无监督主题发现，优于传统LDA，为研究斯里兰卡政治话语的时间演化提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Sri Lankan parliamentary debates (Hansards) constitute a trilingual corpus of speeches in Sinhala, Tamil, and English, including code-mixed content, yet remain inaccessible to standard NLP pipelines due to layout-complex PDFs, multilingual scripts, and agglutinative morphology. We present an end-to-end framework that addresses these challenges through LLM-based text extraction followed by a multilingual embedding and density-based clustering pipeline for topic modeling. A hybrid semantic-lexical extension, BiTopic, is further explored to improve interpretability and recover speeches otherwise discarded as noise. Applied to 19,553 speeches spanning 2017-2026, the pipeline recovers 30 macro-topics achieving a cluster purity (BCP) of 0.673, whose temporal trajectories align unsupervised with major national events including the 2019 Easter Sunday attacks and the 2022 economic crisis. Traditional LDA fails on this corpus due to cross-lingual fragmentation, whereas the proposed approach successfully identifies thematic structure across all three languages without supervision.

</details>


### [99] [Research Paper Quality Recognition Through Textual Feature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20368)
*Saikiran Korla,Sadwik Gummadavelli,Trung-Nghia Le,Minh-Triet Tran,Tam V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于将研究论文分类为"优质"（高被引）和"非优质"（已撤稿）的基准，仅使用标题和摘要的文本特征。实验表明，FastText+SVM组合达到91.12%准确率，SBERT+神经网络达到87.22%准确率，证明了文本信息在评估研究质量方面的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究的质量和可信度塑造着知识与创新，但区分有影响力的高质量工作与有缺陷的研究仍然是一大挑战，因此需要一种仅基于文本特征的方法来评估论文质量。

Method: 将论文分类为两类（高被引=优质，已撤稿=非优质），采用多种嵌入技术（SBERT、Word2Vec、FastText、USE、TF-IDF），并结合多种分类器（SVM、随机森林、神经网络），同时贡献包括：(1)超参数透明化、(2)基于t-SNE的特征空间可视化、(3)基于SHAP的模型可解释性分析、(4)详细的错误案例检验。

Result: 实验结果显示，基于SBERT嵌入的神经网络达到87.22%的准确率，而FastText结合SVM达到91.12%的准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了文本信息在评估研究质量方面的价值，并讨论了部署中的伦理考量，这项工作有助于开发促进可信学术研究的学术诚信工具。

Abstract: Knowledge and innovations are shaped by using the quality and credibility of the scientific research. Yet, distinguishing between impactful, high-quality work and flawed studies remains a challenge. This paper introduces a benchmark for classifying research papers into two categories: good (highly cited) and non-good (retracted), using only textual features from titles and abstracts. We evaluate multiple embedding techniques, including SBERT, Word2Vec, FastText, USE, and TF-IDF, combined with classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and Neural Networks. Our contributions include: (1) hyperparameter transparency, (2) feature space visualizations using t-SNE, (3) model interpretability analysis with SHAP, and (4) detailed examination of error cases. Experimental results show that a neural network with SBERT embeddings achieves 87.22\% accuracy, while FastText combined with SVM reaches 91.12\%. These findings highlight the value of textual information in assessing research quality, with ethical considerations for deployment. This work contributes toward the development of academic integrity tools that promote trustworthy scholarship.

</details>


### [100] [ASTAR: Automated induction of STAndardized radiology Reporting templates from large-scale clinical free-text corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20369)
*Xinfeng Zhang,Mingxuan Liu,Yifei Chen,Juncheng Zhu,Kasidit Anmahapong,Yiming Huang,Yuan Zhang,Hongjia Yang,Yi Liao,Gang Ning,Haibo Qu,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASTAR，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，用于从大规模临床自由文本放射报告中自动归纳标准化放射报告模板，替代传统依赖专家共识的人工模板构建流程。在来自多中心的 4,215 份胎儿脑 MRI 报告上，ASTAR 诱导的模板在模板覆盖率、信息保真度、诊断保真度和专家评分可用性上均优于两份专家人工模板，并将模板开发时间从数周的委员会讨论缩短至数小时的自动化处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化报告将自由文本放射学叙述转换为可查询的数据键，支持队列构建、纵向追踪和医学 AI 训练标签生成。现有主流范式采用两阶段流程：先构建报告模板，再进行信息抽取填充。虽然抽取阶段受益于 LLM 的进展，但模板构建仍依赖劳动密集的专家共识，这一瓶颈既静态、难以扩展，又可能无法覆盖真实世界的报告多样性。

Method: ASTAR 是一个基于 LLM 的自动归纳框架，直接从大规模临床自由文本语料库中归纳标准化放射报告模板（Automated induction of STAndardized radiology Reporting templates）。该框架以数据驱动方式自动生成模板，取代人工专家委员会的设计流程。实验在来自多个中心的 4,215 份胎儿脑 MRI 报告上进行。

Result: 在 4,215 份多中心胎儿脑 MRI 报告上的广泛实验表明，ASTAR 诱导的模板在模板覆盖率、信息保真度、诊断保真度和专家评分可用性四项指标上均超过两份专家人工设计的模板；同时将模板开发时间从数周的委员会审议缩短至数小时的自动化流程。

Conclusion: ASTAR 证明了利用 LLM 自动归纳放射报告模板的可行性，克服了人工模板构建在可扩展性、动态性和真实世界多样性覆盖方面的局限，显著提升了结构化报告流水线的效率与质量。代码已开源（https://github.com/birthlab/ASTAR）。

Abstract: Structured reporting converts free-text radiology narratives into queryable data keys, facilitating cohort assembly, longitudinal tracking, and training label generation for medical AI. The prevailing paradigm follows a two-stage pipeline: (1) constructing a reporting template, (2) extracting information to populate it. While the extraction stage has benefited from advances in large language models (LLMs), template construction remains a manual bottleneck relying on labor-intensive expert consensus that is static, difficult to scale, and may fail to capture real-world reporting diversity. We address this limitation with \textbf{\texttt{ASTAR}}, an LLM-based framework for Automated induction of STAndardized radiology Reporting templates from large-scale clinical free-text corpora. Extensive experiments on 4,215 fetal brain MRI reports from multiple centers demonstrate that the \textbf{\texttt{ASTAR}}-induced template surpasses two expert-curated templates across template coverage, information fidelity, diagnostic fidelity, and expert-rated usability, reducing template development from weeks of committee deliberation to hours of automated processing. Code: https://github.com/birthlab/ASTAR

</details>


### [101] [When Do LLMs Replace Fine-Tuned NLU? A Decision Framework for Intent Detection in Production Conversational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20371)
*Carson Rodrigues,Oysturn Vas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以实验检验“零样本大语言模型（LLM）可替代微调NLU分类器用于意图检测”这一常见论断，发现答案取决于意图空间：在有充足领域内标注的ATIS数据集上，微调RoBERTa以95.9%准确率显著优于Claude Haiku零样本（84.1%），且成本和速度低三个数量级；而在包含150个意图的CLINC150宽泛意图体系上两者统计上打平（89.1 vs 88.5）。LLM的优势体现在三个生产场景：越界（OOS）检测、真实ASR噪声鲁棒性、以及按部署动态变化的意图体系，最终作者将这些发现蒸馏为面向从业者的决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界与业界普遍宣称零样本LLM可以取代微调的NLU分类器完成意图检测，但这一论断缺乏严格、可复现的头对头实证检验。作者希望用严谨的统计方法（bootstrap置信区间、配对显著性检验）在多个数据集和多个真实生产场景下验证该论断，并给出在不同意图空间、数据条件和部署约束下究竟该选哪种方案的可操作指导。

Method: 在ATIS（完整数据集）和CLINC150两个数据集上进行四种方法的头对头对比：微调的RoBERTa、TF-IDF+逻辑回归基线、句子嵌入kNN、Claude Haiku零样本；报告bootstrap 95%置信区间并做配对显著性检验。此外设计了三个面向生产场景的实验：越界（OOS）检测、通过受控的“文本到语音→加噪→Whisper转写”流水线模拟真实ASR噪声的鲁棒性测试，以及跨应用部署（在一个应用意图上训练的分类器迁移到新应用意图）的动态意图体系测试。最终据结果提炼决策框架。

Result: 在ATIS上，微调RoBERTa以95.9%比84.1%领先Claude零样本11.8个百分点（p<0.001），且便宜、快三个数量级；在CLINC150上两者统计上无显著差异（89.1 vs 88.5，p=0.24），即LLM零样本可匹配全监督模型。LLM在三个生产相关场景中占优：OOS召回85.6%对RoBERTa的58.1%；在0 dB的ASR噪声下准确率92.5%对80.0%；在动态逐部署意图体系下，针对旧应用意图训练的分类器对新应用意图得分为0%，而schema提示的LLM对两者均达到约94%，无需再训练。

Conclusion: “零样本LLM是否取代微调分类器”没有普适答案，而是取决于意图空间的规模与结构、是否有充足的领域内标注、以及具体部署需求（OOS检测、噪声鲁棒性、动态schema）。当领域标注充足且意图体系窄时，微调模型在性能、成本与速度上全面占优；当意图体系宽泛、标注稀缺、或面对OOS与动态部署时，零样本LLM更具优势。作者据此为从业者提供了一套实证支撑的决策框架。

Abstract: A common claim is that zero-shot large language models (LLMs) can replace fine-tuned NLU classifiers for intent detection. We test this claim head-to-head and find that the honest answer is: it depends on the intent space. On full ATIS and CLINC150 we compare a fine-tuned RoBERTa, a TF-IDF+logistic-regression baseline, sentence-embedding kNN, and Claude Haiku zero-shot, reporting bootstrap 95% confidence intervals and paired significance tests. When abundant in-domain labels exist, fine-tuned RoBERTa is as good or better and three orders of magnitude cheaper and faster: on ATIS it beats Claude zero-shot by 11.8 points (95.9 vs. 84.1, p<0.001). On the broad 150-intent CLINC150 schema the two are statistically tied (89.1 vs. 88.5, p=0.24): the LLM matches a fully supervised model with no training data. The LLM's advantages appear in three production-relevant regimes: out-of-scope detection (OOS recall 85.6 vs. 58.1 for RoBERTa); robustness to realistic ASR noise via a controlled text-to-speech to noise to Whisper pipeline (92.5 vs. 80.0 at 0 dB); and dynamic per-deployment schemas, where a classifier trained on one app's intents scores 0% on a new app's intents while the schema-prompted LLM serves both at ~94% with zero retraining. We distill these findings into a decision framework for practitioners.

</details>


### [102] [An ambiguity taxonomy for evaluating large language model performance on clinical registry abstraction: a multi-site prospective study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20373)
*James Matheson,Betsy Castillo,Andrew Y. Shin,David Scheinker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（LLM）在未处理的电子病历（EMR）数据上回答临床注册登记（ACC NCDR）问题的能力。经两中心研究（试点+验证），在4,715个共识答案上，人工抽象员间一致性约98%，而LLM答案与共识完全一致者仅87%。问题级平均准确率为91.5%，且随问题所需模糊度和临床推理程度的增加而稳定下降（从药物/事件标志类96%降至事件时间类62%），结论为LLM在未处理EMR数据上的准确率远低于人类抽象员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床注册登记数据抽象通常依赖人工审阅电子病历，耗时费力且成本高昂。作者希望评估LLM能否直接在未处理的原始EMR数据上完成注册登记问题回答，以减轻人工负担并提高效率，同时系统考察不同问题类型（按模糊度与临床推理需求分层）下LLM的表现。

Method: 两阶段设计：（1）试点研究在一家学术医疗中心进行，LLM先为每个注册登记问题识别候选数据源（各问题平均候选文档数14.6~89.2个），经验丰富的抽象员据此定义问题专属文档集；（2）验证研究在第二家中心针对另一个ACC NCDR注册登记进行，LLM依据问题专属文档集回答问题。两位抽象员在查看输出前独立建立金标准，并将每个问题按解决所需的模糊度和临床推理程度分为六类：药物/事件标志、二元临床存在性、行政类、定量实验室/生理类、临床解读类、事件时间类。

Result: 分析样本含9,430个抽象员答案，调和为4,715个共识答案（试点501，验证4,214）。验证阶段人工评分者间一致性约98%，LLM答案87%与共识完全一致、2%部分一致、9%不一致；在至少20个答案的157个问题上，问题级平均准确率91.5%（SD 13.4%），且随模糊度增加而从药物/事件标志类的96%降至事件时间类的62%。

Conclusion: 在未处理的EMR数据上，LLM回答临床注册登记问题的准确率远低于人类抽象员，且准确率随问题所需模糊度和临床推理程度的增加而稳定下降。这表明LLM尚不能直接替代人工进行注册登记数据抽象，尤其对高模糊度和强临床推理需求（如事件时间判定）的问题，需进一步改进或结合人工审核。

Abstract: Objective: To evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on unprocessed electronic medical record (EMR) data for clinical registry abstraction. Methods: We evaluated LLM performance answering registry questions for the American College of Cardiology National Cardiovascular Data Registry (ACC NCDR). In a pilot study at an academic medical center, the model identified candidate data sources for each registry question and experienced abstractors used these results to define question-specific document sets. In a validation study at a second center with a second ACC NCDR registry, the LLM answered questions using the question-specific document sets. Before reviewing any output, two abstractors independently established the ground truth and assigned each question to one of six categories, ordered by the ambiguity and clinical reasoning required to resolve it: Medication/Event Flag, Binary Clinical Presence, Administrative, Quantitative Laboratory/Physiologic, Clinical Interpretation, and Event Timing. Results: The analytical sample comprised 9,430 abstractor answers reconciled to 4,715 consensus answers (501 pilot; 4,214 validation). In the pilot, candidate data sources per question averaged between 14.6 (SD 13.9) for demographics and 89.2 (SD 56.1) for history and risk factors. In validation, human inter-rater agreement was approximately 98\% while 87\% of LLM answers exactly matched consensus, 2\% partially, and 9\% did not. Mean question-level accuracy was 91.5\% (SD 13.4\%) across 157 questions with at least 20 answers, and declined as ambiguity increased, from 96\% for Medication/Event Flag to 62\% for Event Timing questions. Conclusions: LLMs answering clinical registry questions on unprocessed EMR data achieved far lower accuracy than human abstractors. LLM accuracy fell steadily as ambiguity and the level of required clinical reasoning increased.

</details>


### [103] [VA-DPO: Valence-Arousal Direct Preference Optimization for Controllable Emotion Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20374)
*Hyunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 VA-DPO，一种通过连续 Valence-Arousal 平面坐标（v*, a*）来精确控制语言模型情感生成的方法。它仅修改 DPO 的偏好数据构造方式：用冻结的 VA 回归器按与目标的欧氏距离为生成样本打分，保留距离差超过阈值 τ 的候选对，再用标准 DPO 损失在冻结参考模型上优化 LoRA 适配器。在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上平均 VA 距离较系统提示降低 33%、较少样本提示降低 25%，效价/唤醒相关系数分别达 r_v=0.93 与 r_a=0.75，且在 Qwen3-8B 与 Llama-3.2-3B 上表现稳定，MMLU 不变、HellaSwag 与 TruthfulQA 保持，并开源了代码、配置与偏好构造流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感生成工作大多使用离散情感标签（如 happy, angry, sad），无法表达“轻微沮丧但平静”这类细微的连续情感目标。作者希望以连续方式指定期望情感，以突破离散标签的表达局限。

Method: 将期望情感指定为 V-A 平面上的连续点 (v*, a*)，并对 DPO 做小幅修改（VA-DPO）：用冻结的 VA 回归器按生成样本与目标的欧氏距离打分，仅保留距离差超过裕度 τ 的候选对作为偏好数据，随后以标准 DPO 损失配合冻结参考模型优化 LoRA 适配器。DPO 目标本身不变，创新点在于偏好数据的构建方式。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上，VA-DPO 将平均 VA 距离相对系统提示降低 33%、相对少样本提示降低 25%，效价/唤醒相关性提升至 r_v=0.93 与 r_a=0.75；收益可迁移到 Qwen3-8B 与 Llama-3.2-3B。同时不牺牲通用能力：MMLU 不变（Δ=+0.0），HellaSwag 与 TruthfulQA 保持原有水平。

Conclusion: VA-DPO 证明以连续 V-A 坐标作为情感目标可以显著提升情感可控性，且其收益具有跨模型泛化性，不损害一般能力基准。作者公开代码、配置与偏好构造流程，便于复现与后续研究。

Abstract: How precisely can we tell a language model how to feel? Most work on emotional generation answers with a discrete label - happy, angry, sad - which cannot express a target like "mildly downcast but calm." We instead specify the desired affect as a continuous point (v*, a*) in the Valence-Arousal plane and train the model to hit it. Our method, VA-DPO, is a small modification to Direct Preference Optimization: a frozen VA regressor scores each sampled generation by its Euclidean distance to the target, we keep only candidate pairs whose distance gap clears a margin tau, and we optimize a LoRA adapter with the ordinary DPO loss against a frozen reference. The DPO objective itself is unchanged; what is new is how the preference data is built. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct this cuts mean VA distance to the target by 33% over system-prompting and 25% over few-shot prompting, lifting valence/arousal correlation to r_v=0.93 and r_a=0.75. The gains carry over to Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.2-3B, and they do not come at the usual price: MMLU is unchanged (Delta=+0.0) and HellaSwag and TruthfulQA are preserved. We release the code, configs, and the preference-construction pipeline.

</details>


### [104] [GRAFT: Adaptive DLM-Based Draft Tree Construction with Target-Distilled Edge Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20375)
*Xuming Ye,Zeming Ma,Runjie Yu,Yuan Liu,Tianle Li,Shuhan Bai,Jian Zhou,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GRAFT，一种面向扩散语言模型（DLM）投机解码的草稿树构建框架，通过目标蒸馏边评分（TDES）建模父-子兼容性以替代仅依赖概率的边选择，并通过状态感知预算分配（SABA）动态设定每轮树预算，兼顾草稿增益与验证开销，实现2.13–6.36倍端到端加速且每轮仅增加不到0.5ms开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于树的投机解码通过验证多条草稿路径提升平均接受token数，但现有树构建依赖父条件扩展，不兼容DLM草稿模型（如DFlash）单次前向即输出所有未来位置分布的特性；现有DDTree虽将高概率token作为候选节点，但其边选择仅依赖token概率而忽视父-子兼容性，且固定预算无法适配吞吐最优树大小随解码状态变化的特点。

Method: GRAFT包含两大模块：1) 目标蒸馏边评分（TDES），从目标模型推理痕迹中蒸馏父-子偏好，据此选择与目标模型兼容的边；2) 状态感知预算分配（SABA），通过平衡预期草稿增益与验证成本，按轮动态设定草稿树大小预算。

Result: 在多种模型与任务上，GRAFT相较自回归解码实现2.13–6.36倍端到端加速，每轮新增开销小于0.5ms，约为目标模型验证延迟的1.4%。

Conclusion: GRAFT有效解决了DLM草稿场景下树边选择缺乏父-子兼容性建模以及固定预算分配不合理的问题，以极低额外开销换取显著的端到端解码加速，可用于基于DLM的投机解码框架。

Abstract: Tree-based speculative decoding raises the mean accepted tokens of standard speculative decoding by verifying multiple draft paths, and existing tree builders typically construct these paths through parent-conditioned expansion, where each child token is generated conditioned on its parent path. This construction is incompatible with diffusion language model (DLM) drafters such as DFlash, which produces all future-position distributions in a single forward pass. DDTree bridges this gap by treating high-probability tokens from each future-position distribution as candidate nodes and selecting edges between consecutive positions under a fixed node budget. However, its edge selection relies on token probability alone without modeling parent--child compatibility, so target-compatible tokens can be attached to wrong parents; moreover, its fixed budget ignores that the throughput-optimal tree size varies with the decoding state. We propose GRAFT, a draft-tree construction framework for DLM-based speculative decoding. GRAFT introduces Target-Distilled Edge Scoring (TDES), which distills parent--child preferences from target-model traces to select target-compatible edges, and State-Aware Budget Allocation (SABA), which sets the per-round tree budget by balancing expected draft gain against verification cost. Across multiple models and tasks, GRAFT achieves $2.13\times$--$6.36\times$ end-to-end speedup over autoregressive decoding while adding less than $0.5$\,ms of overhead per round, approximately $1.4\%$ of the target-model verification latency.

</details>


### [105] [TH-GNN: Heterogeneous Temporal Graph Neural Networks for LLM-Agent Shilling Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20376)
*Shivam Swarup,Divya Prakash Shrivastava,Rakesh Thakur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TH-GNN——一种异构时序图神经网络，同时建模时间、结构与语义信号，用于检测LLM驱动的推荐系统托攻击（shilling attack）。模型以双层异构图Transformer为骨干，结合可学习正弦时间编码、跨模态注意力（融合图结构与RoBERTa文本表征）以及基于GRU的时间突发性建模，在5类攻击族与4个基准数据集上达到加权平均F1 0.870，显著优于最强文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器存在明显的模态缺陷：纯文本检测器仅捕捉评论文本的语义漂移，无法感知图结构与时间协同性；纯图检测器虽能利用邻居异常，但无法理解评论语义及LLM生成内容带来的跨模态不一致性。随着LLM能以规模化方式生成逼真的托攻击画像、流畅评论和一致的评分，现有防御系统性失效，因此需要联合建模多模态信号的检测框架。

Method: TH-GNN由三个核心组件构成：(1) 双层异构图Transformer骨干，对不同类型的节点和关系采用分别的注意力机制，并在每条边上加入可学习的正弦时间编码；(2) 跨模态注意力，将结构化的用户嵌入与冻结的RoBERTa对评论和物品描述的语义表示进行融合；(3) 在log到达时间间隔上运行的GRU，捕捉攻击的时间突发性（burstiness）。整体实现对时序、结构与语义三类信号的联合建模。

Result: 在5种攻击族和4个基准数据集上进行评估，TH-GNN取得加权平均F1分数0.870。在Agent4SR攻击上，较最强文本基线提升10.9个百分点；在最低注入率（最困难）场景下提升11.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验证明，联合建模时间、结构和语义信号能有效检测复杂精密的LLM驱动托攻击，弥补了单一模态检测器的盲区，为推荐系统对抗LLM生成攻击提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM agents can now generate realistic shilling profiles, fluent reviews, and coherent ratings at scale, systematically defeating recommender-system defenses. Text-only detectors that flag semantic drift in review embeddings are blind to graph structure and temporal coordination, while graph-only detectors that exploit neighborhood anomalies cannot reason over review semantics or the cross-modal inconsistencies produced by LLM-generated content. We propose TH-GNN, a heterogeneous temporal graph neural network with a two-layer Heterogeneous Graph Transformer backbone that applies per-type and per-relation attention augmented with learnable sinusoidal temporal encodings on every edge. Cross-modal attention fuses structural user embeddings with frozen RoBERTa representations of reviews and item descriptions, while a GRU operating over log inter-arrival times captures temporal burstiness. Evaluated across five attack families and four benchmark datasets, TH-GNN achieves a grand-mean F1 score of 0.870, outperforming the strongest text-only baseline on Agent4SR attacks by 10.9 percentage points and 11.5 percentage points at the lowest injection rate. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly modeling temporal, structural, and semantic signals for detecting sophisticated LLM-driven shilling attacks.

</details>


### [106] [EditPPT: Faithful Long-Deck Slide Editing via Structured Tool-Using Multi-Agent with Dual-Modal Validators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20381)
*Jiheon Kim,Kyudan Jung,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EditPPT，一种将幻灯片编辑重构为受限工具选择问题的多智能体框架。它通过原生 PowerPoint COM 接口执行局部形状级操作，缩小 LLM 动作空间并保留用户原稿结构；配合跨模态双模态验证提升指令遵循与视觉质量评估的鲁棒性。同时发布 DeckEdit-Bench 基准（28 份人工撰写幻灯片、582 页、183 个编辑提示，覆盖短/中/长三种长度）。实验表明 EditPPT 在整体及长 deck 上均表现优异（执行率 99.5%、slide 定位 F1 88.7%、指令遵循 82.5%、对象保留 91.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的幻灯片编辑系统在真实演示文件中表现不佳：它们依赖理想化的中间表示或开放式代码生成，在长 deck 中容易产生级联错误。因此需要一个既能保证修改准确性、又能保持原稿保真度、且对 deck 长度具有鲁棒性的自动化编辑方案。

Method: EditPPT 采用多智能体框架，将幻灯片编辑形式化为受限的“工具选择”问题：通过原生 PowerPoint COM 接口执行局部形状级操作，从而收窄 LLM 的动作空间并利用应用层已解析的结构；同时采用跨模态（双模态）验证机制，将指令遵循度与视觉质量分开评估，以获得更稳健的验证结果；并构建 DeckEdit-Bench 基准，按短、中、长三个梯队评测。

Result: 在 DeckEdit-Bench 上，EditPPT 整体达到 99.5% 的执行率、88.7% 的 slide 定位 F1、82.5% 的指令遵循率、91.5% 的对象保留率，并且在长 deck 上也保持了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过将编辑任务约束为受限的工具选择并使用原生接口执行形状级操作，EditPPT 有效缓解了长 deck 中级联错误问题，在准确性与保真度之间取得良好平衡；代码与基准已公开，为后续幻灯片自动编辑研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Automating slide editing requires simultaneously satisfying modification accuracy, preservation fidelity, and robustness to deck length. Existing LLM-based systems often fail on real-world presentation files because they rely on idealized intermediate representations or open-ended code generation, which are prone to cascading errors in long decks. We introduce EditPPT, a multi-agent framework that reformulates slide editing as a constrained tool-selection problem. By executing localized shape-level operations through the native PowerPoint COM interface, EditPPT narrows the LLM action space while preserving the application-resolved structure of user-authored decks. By separating validation across modalities, our dual-modal validation provides more robust assessment of both instruction fidelity and visual quality. We also present DeckEdit-Bench, a benchmark with 28 human-authored decks, 582 slides, and 183 editing prompts across short, medium, and long deck tiers. Experiments show that EditPPT achieves a 99.5% execution rate, 88.7% slide-targeting F1, 82.5% instruction following, and 91.5% object preservation overall, while maintaining strong performance on long decks. Our code and benchmark are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EditPPT-0E27/

</details>


### [107] [Decoupled Vision-Language System for Multimodal Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20382)
*Yifan Xu,Baochen Xiong,Xiaoshan Yang,Donglin Di,Yaowei Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Libra，一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的新架构，同时支持多模态理解与生成。通过视觉系统与语言系统之间的跨模态桥接，配合开关注意力（switch attention）和开关 FFN 模块实现自模态建模与跨模态交互的解耦，并在两种设置（Libra-1 仅图像到文本理解；Libra-2 统一理解与文本到图像生成）下验证，实验表明该设计能使理解与生成相互促进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在理解任务上表现优异，但统一理解与生成仍具挑战。现有架构往往将自模态建模与跨模态交互耦合在一起，导致各模态难以学习自身独有的表示，同时跨模态理解效率受限。作者希望通过架构层面的解耦，让各模态既能学习独特特征，又能保持有效的跨模态理解，从而使理解与生成达成双向互利。

Method: Libra 架构包含一个视觉系统和一个语言系统，二者通过跨模态桥接连接。核心解耦机制包括：（1）开关注意力模块（switch attention）与（2）开关 FFN 模块，它们根据计算场景动态路由计算流——即区分自模态建模与跨模态交互场景。此外，论文还讨论了在 tokenization（词元化）、位置编码和监督方案上的改进，并针对理解任务（Libra-1）与统一理解-生成任务（Libra-2）分别设计设置。

Result: 实验表明，Libra 的专用架构设计能够使多模态理解与生成相互促进，在理解基准与生成基准上均取得强性能，证明了模态解耦架构在统一多模态任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: Libra 验证了通过架构级解耦（自模态建模 vs 跨模态交互）可以在单个模型中同时实现出色的多模态理解与生成能力，为后续统一多模态大模型的设计提供了新的架构方向，涵盖注意力路由、前馈网络、词元化与位置编码等多个层面的改进。

Abstract: We introduce a new architecture design for multimodal large language models (MLLMs), Libra, capable of both multimodal understanding and generation. Libra architecture contains one vision system and one language system, connected by cross-modal bridges. This design decouples self-modal modeling and cross-modal interaction, enabling each modality to learn its unique representations while maintaining effective cross-modal comprehension. The decoupling is mainly achieved in a switch attention module and a switch FFN module, which dynamically routes the computation flow for self-modal modeling and cross-modal interaction scenarios. We evaluate the effectiveness in two important settings: \textbf{Libra-1} for the understanding-only image-to-text setting, and \textbf{Libra-2} for unified image-to-text understanding and text-to-image generation. In addition to the architecture design, we discuss various improvements on tokenization, positional encoding, and supervision. Experiments demonstrate that the dedicated Libra design enables mutual improvements on multimodal understanding and generation, achieving strong performance on both understanding and generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [108] [Using Human-LLM Disagreement to Improve Checklist-Based Quality Appraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20385)
*Timo van der Kuil,Bruno Messina Coimbra,Mirjam van Zuiden,Robert A. Bagheri,Rens van de Schoot,Klaas Dieleman,Berend Greijn,Stefan Houkes,Sebastiaan Rodenhuis,Elizabeth M. Grandfield*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究大语言模型（LLM）在基于清单的系统评价质量评价中能否逼近专家判断，并检验人类-LLM不一致是否可用于识别与改进模糊的清单条目。基于GRoLTS清单、三个研究主题和两个清单版本，发现LLM表现随条目而异，模糊与条件性标准分歧最大；修订这些条目可提升原始一致性与机会校正一致性，且在高一致性条目保留时，LLM评分往往能维持研究级别排序——表明可靠的LLM辅助评价不仅取决于模型选择，也取决于清单设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统评价中纳入研究的质量评价耗时且对清单标准的模糊性敏感。尽管LLM可辅助此类任务，但评价清单通常被视为固定输入，其设计如何影响与专家判断的一致性尚不清楚。本文旨在验证LLM能否近似人类判断，并探索是否可利用人机分歧来识别和改进有问题的清单条目。

Method: 使用GRoLTS清单，将LLM生成的评价与专家标注进行比较，覆盖三个研究主题和两个清单版本。一致性评估采用条目级准确率、机会校正一致性（如Cohen's kappa类指标）以及研究级排序保持情况三种方式。通过修订高分歧的模糊条目形成第二版清单，并对比修订前后的表现。

Result: LLM表现随清单条目差异显著，模糊和条件性标准产生最大的人机分歧；修订这些条目同时提升了原始一致性与机会校正一致性。尽管条目级误判仍然存在，但在保留高一致性条目时，LLM生成的评分通常能保持研究间的相对排序。

Conclusion: 可靠的LLM辅助质量评价不仅依赖模型选择，还依赖清单设计。分析人类与LLM的分歧模式有助于识别存在问题的清单条目，并支持研究综合工作流程的迭代改进。

Abstract: Systematic reviews rely on quality appraisal of included studies, a process that is time-consuming and sensitive to ambiguity in checklist criteria. Although large language models (LLMs) offer opportunities to support these tasks, appraisal checklists are typically treated as fixed inputs, and it remains unclear how their design affects agreement with expert judgments. Therefore, we investigate (1) whether LLMs can approximate human judgments in checklist-based appraisal and (2) whether patterns of human-LLM disagreement can be used to identify and improve ambiguous checklist items. Using the Guidelines for Reporting on Latent Trajectory Studies (GRoLTS) checklist, we compare LLM-generated assessments with expert annotations across three research topics and two checklist versions. Agreement is assessed using item-level accuracy, chance-corrected agreement, and preservation of study-level rank ordering.
  We find that performance varies substantially across checklist items, with ambiguous and conditional criteria producing the greatest disagreement. Revising these items improves both raw and chance-corrected agreement. Although item-level misclassifications persist, LLM-generated scores often preserve the relative ranking of studies when high-agreement items are retained. These results indicate that reliable LLM-assisted appraisal depends not only on model choice but also on checklist design. The findings suggest that analyzing human-LLM disagreement can help identify problematic checklist items and support the iterative improvement of research synthesis workflows.

</details>


### [109] [Poly-InstructTTS: Learning In-the-Wild Expressive Speech Synthesis from Open-Ended Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20387)
*Junhui Zhang,Qianhui Xu,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Ling Miao,Qiangqiang Wang,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Poly-InstructTTS，一个通过开放式自然语言指令控制细粒度语音情感与风格表达的 TTS 系统。它利用野外音视频数据，经可扩展的多模态流程构建了约 1,000 小时的指令标注语料库，覆盖 1,000 余种细粒度情感与风格；框架采用基于属性的思考标记（thinking tokens）的免提示 GPT 主干，配合流匹配模块（flow matching）注入参考音频的音色。此外提出说话人微调流程，在保持人设的前提下将指令控制迁移到特定说话人，并扩展了 InstructTTSEval 评测集。实验表明该系统在指令遵循度和表现力上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 模型虽已具备较高自然度，但通过自然语言指令精细控制语音中的情感表达仍具挑战性。作者旨在突破指令范围有限、无法覆盖开放式中细粒度情感与风格的问题，并解决大规模指令标注数据稀缺的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建可扩展的多模态数据管线，从野外音视频数据自动生成 1,000 小时、覆盖 1,000+ 细粒度情感/风格的指令标注语料；2) 提出免提示 GPT 架构，以属性化思考标记（thinking tokens）隐式推理表达属性；3) 引入流匹配模块从参考音频注入音色，实现跨说话人表达控制；4) 设计说话人微调流程，将指令控制适配到特定说话人而不损失其人设一致性；5) 扩展 InstructTTSEval 评测任务以进行更全面的评估。

Result: 实验表明 Poly-InstructTTS 在指令遵循（instruction adherence）与表达力（expressiveness）两方面均取得优异性能；同时提供了音频示例与扩展后的测试集。

Conclusion: Poly-InstructTTS 验证了利用野外音视觉大数据与属性级隐式推理（thinking tokens）可显著提升开放式指令控制的细粒度语音表达，且说话人微调流程保证了人设保持与指令可控之间的兼顾，为构建更具表现力、更可控的 TTS 系统提供了可行范式。

Abstract: While recent text-to-speech (TTS) models achieve high naturalness, controlling fine-grained expression via natural-language instructions remains challenging. We introduce Poly- InstructTTS, which learns expressive speech from open-ended instructions using in-the-wild audiovisual data. We build a scalable multi-modal pipeline to construct a 1,000-hour instruction-annotated corpus covering 1,000+ fine-grained emotions and styles. The framework uses a prompt-free GPT with attribute-based thinking tokens, followed by a flow-matching module that injects timbre from a reference audio. We also present a speaker fine-tuning procedure to transfer instruction control to specific speakers while preserving persona. We further extend InstructTTSEval with broader tasks. Experiments show that Poly-InstructTTS delivers strong performance in instruction adherence and expressiveness. Audio demos and the expanded testset are available on our project page.

</details>


### [110] [Intent Engine: Natural-Language Intent Translation for Intent-Driven Orchestration in the Compute Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20388)
*Koushikur Islam,Rodrigo N. Calheiros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Intent Engine，一种将自然语言意图转换为已验证 SLO（服务级目标）工件的中介架构，用于计算连续体中的微服务部署。它通过 schema 约束抽取、基于检索的取值构建和约束验证，解决了 LLM 直接生成 SLO 工件时的不受支持约束、错误取值和 schema 违规问题，显著降低了下游部署失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算连续体中的微服务部署由底层 SLO 驱动，但要求用户指定指标级约束会造成采用障碍并增加误配置风险。LLM 虽能理解自然语言意图，但直接生成可供编排使用的 SLO 工件仍不可靠，会出现不受支持的约束、错误的接地取值和 schema 违规，这些错误会传播到下游部署逻辑，产生不可行或错误的部署结果。

Method: Intent Engine 作为意图获取与 SLO 构建层，置于现有意图驱动编排与部署框架之前，本身不执行部署或运行时 QoS 优化。其架构结合三种机制：schema 约束抽取、从监控的基础设施状态进行检索接地取值构建，以及在输出最终 SLO 工件之前对受支持约束进行验证。

Result: 在基于边缘-云测试平台派生的 716 条意图到 SLO 数据集中（含有效与无效意图），Intent Engine 在 GPT-4.1 mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5 和 DeepSeek V4-Flash 上均优于 prompting 基线和基于规则的非 LLM 解析器。使用 GPT-4.1 mini 时，总 F1 分数达 0.941，聚合幻觉降低 85.1%，下游部署失败率从 30.8% 降至 2.1%。

Conclusion: Intent Engine 通过受约束的 LLM 翻译与验证流水线，有效弥合了自然语言意图与编排可用 SLO 工件之间的鸿沟，显著提高了 SLO 工件的正确性并大幅降低下游部署失败率，从而降低采用门槛和误配置风险。

Abstract: Microservice placement in the compute continuum is driven by low-level Service-level Objectives (SLOs), but requiring users to specify metric-level constraints creates an adoption barrier and increases misconfiguration risk. Although large language models (LLMs) can interpret natural-language intents, direct generation of orchestration-consumable SLO artifacts remains unreliable due to unsupported constraints, incorrect grounded values, and schema violations. These errors can propagate to downstream placement logic and produce infeasible or incorrect placements. This paper presents Intent Engine, a natural-language intent translation architecture that constructs validated SLO artifacts for compute-continuum service placement. Intent Engine acts as an intent acquisition and SLO construction layer for existing intent-driven orchestration and placement frameworks; it does not perform placement or runtime QoS optimization. The architecture combines schema-constrained extraction, retrieval-grounded value construction from monitored infrastructure state, and validation against supported constraints before emitting the final SLO artifact. We evaluate Intent Engine using a 716-record intent-to-SLO dataset derived from an edge-cloud testbed, including valid and invalid intents. Across GPT-4.1 mini, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and DeepSeek V4-Flash, Intent Engine outperforms prompting baselines and a non-LLM rule-based parser. With GPT-4.1 mini, it achieves 0.941 total F1 Score and reduces aggregate hallucination by 85.1%, while lowering downstream placement failure from 30.8% to 2.1%.

</details>


### [111] [Ansari: A Retrieval-Grounded Islamic AI Assistant -- Architecture, Deployment, and Lessons from 140,000 Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20390)
*M Waleed Kadous,Amr Elsayed,Abdullah Al Nahas,Ashraf Haress*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Ansari，一个已部署的、基于检索增强的伊斯兰 AI 助手，自 2023 年 6 月以来已在 25 种以上语言中处理了超过 14 万次对话。系统通过代理式检索循环，让语言模型仅基于从经过认证的伊斯兰语料库（《古兰经》、圣训集、教法学百科全书和经注学资料）检索到的内容作答并附带引用，以解决通用大语言模型在宗教问题上的事实编造与价值观错位两大风险。论文描述了系统架构、多平台部署、真实用户行为，并通过多项评估（机构考试零样本性能、斋月期间人工评级、外部基准）展示了其在 IslamicMMLU 上领先前沿模型、在伊斯兰法律推理上具有竞争力且强抗虚假前提的表现，最后提炼出对任何信仰或价值观敏感场景下 LLM 部署的通用教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型日益被用于回答宗教问题，但在伊斯兰内容上存在两大严重风险：事实性编造（虚构《古兰经》经文或圣训）以及微妙的价值错位。作者旨在构建一个可实际部署的、能可靠且贴合伊斯兰教义伦理的 AI 助手，并从真实部署数据中总结出可迁移的部署经验。

Method: Ansari 的核心是代理式检索循环（agentic retrieval loop）：使用工具的语言模型针对经过认证的伊斯兰语料库（《古兰经》、圣训集、多卷本教法学百科全书、经注学资料）发起搜索，并且仅依据检索到的内容作答，附带引用供核验。系统提示词编码了编辑与神学政策。系统部署于网页、移动端、WhatsApp，并作为 Model Context Protocol 服务器和 Agent Skill 提供。评估方法包括：认证机构考试上的零样本性能、斋月期间的人工评级验证、以及两个独立外部运行基准（IslamicMMLU 与 IslamicLegalBench）。

Result: Ansari 在公开的 IslamicMMLU 排行榜上领先于前沿模型，在 IslamicLegalBench 上的伊斯兰法律推理方面具有竞争力，并强有力地抵抗虚假前提（false premises）；斋月期间的人工验证与机构考试评估同样表现良好。140,000 次真实对话揭示了穆斯林实际使用此类工具的方式。

Conclusion: 作者提炼出超越伊斯兰领域、适用于任何信仰或价值观敏感 LLM 部署的通用教训：检索落地（grounding）是必要但不充分的；系统提示词既是技术工件也是神学工件；模型形成过程中缺乏社区参与仍然是一个难以弥合的差距。Ansari 的实践表明，检索落地加引用可显著缓解编造与错位风险，但人工/社区维度的缺失仍是根本性硬约束。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer religious questions, but for Islamic content they carry two serious risks: factual fabrication (inventing Qur'anic verses or hadith) and subtle value misalignment. We present Ansari, a deployed, retrieval-grounded Islamic AI assistant that has handled more than 140,000 conversations across 25+ languages since June 2023. Ansari is built around an agentic retrieval loop: a tool-using language model issues searches against authenticated Islamic corpora -- the Qur'an, hadith collections, a multi-volume jurisprudence (fiqh) encyclopedia, and exegetical (tafsir) sources -- and answers only on the basis of what it retrieves, with citations attached for verification. We describe the system's architecture (the agent loop, the retrieval tools, the corpora, and the system prompt that encodes editorial and theological policy), its multi-platform deployment (web, mobile, WhatsApp, and as a Model Context Protocol server and an Agent Skill), and what 140,000 real conversations reveal about how Muslims actually use such a tool. We report results on several complementary evaluations -- zero-shot performance on accredited institutional exams, a human-rated validation during Ramadan, and two independent, externally run benchmarks on which Ansari currently tops the public IslamicMMLU leaderboard ahead of frontier models and is competitive on Islamic legal reasoning (IslamicLegalBench) while strongly resisting false premises -- and draw out lessons that generalize beyond Islam to any faith- or values-sensitive deployment of LLMs: grounding is necessary but not sufficient, the system prompt is a theological as much as a technical artifact, and the absence of community in how models are formed remains a hard gap.

</details>


### [112] [ImmigrationReason: A Structured Dataset of U.S. Immigration Appeals for Legal Reasoning Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20391)
*Amirhossein Afsharrad,Seyed Shahabeddin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ImmigrationReason，一个大规模结构化数据集，涵盖 2005-2026 年美国移民局行政上诉办公室（AAO）的 12,375 份非先例裁决。每份记录包含适用法律框架、五项类别下的逐标准证据充分性判定、裁决者批评原话、全部引用及最终处置，并配有经 Claude 转录的高质量源文本。数据经双模态三遍提取管线与 Opus 4.7 比较提示裁决验证，并由领域专家在 500 条样本上核实。该数据集记录了近 9,000 例 AAO 认定的法律错误，跨越 2016 年 Dhanasar 规则变动的自然制度更迭，覆盖 21 年裁决历史，并支撑结果预测、裁决者错误分析与高风险监管领域智能体设计等研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律 NLP 资源大多基于联邦判例法并聚焦粗粒度分类，而行政裁决才是绝大多数政府决策发生的场域，却基本上未被涉及。作者旨在填补这一空白，为行政裁决这一高影响力但资源稀缺的领域提供结构化、细粒度的数据集与语言学资源。

Method: 构建了从 USCIS AAO 12,375 份非先例裁决（2005-2026）中抽取的结构化数据集 ImmigrationReason。每条记录捕获法律框架、五类标签下的逐标准证据充分性判定、裁决者批评的原话引文、全部引用及最终处置。提取质量通过三条通道的管线保证：结合两种独立模态进行提取，并由 Opus 4.7 通过比较提示进行裁决，另在 500 条样本上由领域专家验证。

Result: 数据集包含近 9,000 条 AAO 识别出的法律错误的原话实例，覆盖 21 年的裁决历史，并自然跨越 2016 年 Dhanasar 规则变更这一法律体制转型。作者对数据集进行了详细分析，并验证了提取质量（双模态 + 专家抽检）。

Conclusion: 该数据集为行政裁决的法律 NLP 研究提供了新基础，支持结果预测、裁决者错误分析，以及面向高风险监管领域的高性能智能体（agent）设计等研究方向，为从粗粒度联邦判例分类转向细粒度行政决策分析开辟了道路。

Abstract: Most legal NLP resources draw from federal case law and focus on coarse classification, leaving administrative adjudication, where the vast majority of government decisions occur, essentially unaddressed. We introduce ImmigrationReason, a large-scale structured dataset derived from 12,375 non-precedent decisions of the U.S. Citizenship and Immigration Services (USCIS) Administrative Appeals Office (AAO) spanning 2005 to 2026. Each record captures the applicable legal framework, per-criterion evidence-sufficiency findings under a five-category label, verbatim adjudicator-criticism quotes, all citations, and final dispositions, alongside high-quality Claude-transcribed source text. Extraction quality is validated through a three-pass pipeline combining two independent modalities with comparison-prompt adjudication by Opus 4.7, and verified by domain experts on a 500-record sample. The dataset documents nearly 9,000 verbatim instances of AAO-identified legal errors, spans a natural legal-regime transition (the 2016 Dhanasar rule change), and covers 21 years of adjudication. We analyze the dataset in detail and outline research directions it enables, from outcome prediction and adjudicator-error analysis to agent design for high-stakes regulatory domains.

</details>


### [113] [Evaluation-as-Search: Adaptive Discovery of Grounding Failures in Meeting Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20392)
*Sami Khairy,Yasaman Hosseinkashi,Vishak Gopal,Ross Cutler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Evaluation-as-Search (EaS)，一种将质量评估建模为对自然问题空间的适应性搜索的反馈驱动方法，并据此构建了 MeetingProbe 基准（含 3,000+ 标注问答对，覆盖 20 份会议记录、三个会议类型与三个 LLM 助手）。相比随机探测，自适应搜索的失败发现率提升 2.5 倍（7.1% vs 2.9%），识别出 8 类重复失败，且主要由话语-语用挑战而非事实记忆错误驱动，并在多模型家族与供应商上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 会议助手已大规模部署，但其"grounding 保真度"（语义落地忠实性）缺乏系统性评估；现有静态基准会遗漏与特定话语结构或推理需求相关的失败模式，因此需要更动态、更聚焦的评测方法。

Method: 提出反馈驱动的 EaS 方法论：将评估建模为对"参会者可能提出的自然问题"空间的适应性搜索。不采用均匀采样，而是根据评估者反馈跨迭代学习，依据 UCB 打分覆盖图与盲式多维度质量评估，将探测精力集中在最可能发生失败的认知需求上；据此构建 MeetingProbe 基准。

Result: 自适应搜索的失败发现率是随机探测的 2.5 倍（7.1% vs 2.9%），其中"策略规划者（strategic planner）"贡献最大单一效应；三个模型呈现清晰的能力梯度，并识别出 8 类反复出现的失败，主导类型为话语-语用困难而非事实回忆错误；在多个模型家族与供应商上进一步验证，发现干净的能力梯度及一组无任何模型能处理的普遍失败子集。

Conclusion: EaS 证明了反馈驱动的自适应探测能显著提升会议助手 grounding 保真度的评测效率与覆盖面；MeetingProbe 作为公开基准发布，支持可复现的会议助手评估，为领域提供了系统化的评测方法。

Abstract: LLM-powered meeting assistants are deployed at scale, yet systematic evaluation of their grounding fidelity remains limited to static benchmarks that miss failure modes tied to specific discourse structures or reasoning demands. We propose Evaluation-as-Search (EaS), a feedback-driven methodology that frames quality evaluation as an adaptive search over the space of natural questions a meeting participant might ask. Rather than sampling uniformly, EaS learns from evaluator feedback across iterations to concentrate probing effort on cognitive demands where failures are most likely, guided by a UCB-scored coverage map and blind multi-dimensional quality evaluation. Using EaS, we construct MeetingProbe, a benchmark of over $3{,}000$ annotated question--answer pairs spanning 20 transcripts from three meeting genres and three LLM assistants. In ablations, adaptive search surfaces $2.5\times$ more failures than random probing ($7.1\%$ vs. $2.9\%$ finding rate), with the strategic planner contributing the largest individual effect. Across three models, we observe a clear capability gradient and identify eight recurring failure categories dominated by discourse-pragmatic challenges rather than factual recall errors. We further validate MeetingProbe across multiple model families and providers, finding a clean capability gradient and a curated subset of universal failures that no model handles. MeetingProbe is released publicly to support reproducible evaluation of meeting assistant grounding fidelity.

</details>


### [114] [Knowledge-Graph-Gated Defactualization for Style-Controllable and Fact-Preserving Generation in Agentic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20393)
*Tanmay Kumar Shrivastava,Darsh Rohit Nandu,Rajesh Kumar Mundotiya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DSR（Defactualize-Steer-Rehydrate）是一个将带类型和显著性权重的知识图谱（KG）与激活引导相结合的免微调框架：在引导前用占位符替换显著实体（去事实化），生成后按显著性引导恢复已验证的值（再水合）。在6个LLaMA系列模型（1B-13B）上、600个A2A生成的客服案例（1200次生成）中，DSR相较纯引导基线显著提升已验证实体的恢复率（Cohen's d=0.225, p_Bonf=1.0×10^-4），同时保持有效的风格控制，并揭示了表征级引导与事实接地之间此前未被探索的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在客服等对事实敏感的应用中，Agentic LLM必须同时保持事实正确性并生成可控风格化语体。激活引导虽可免微调地实现风格控制，但缺乏区分可验证事实与风格化内容的显式机制，导致语义泄漏（steering影响事实内容）。作者旨在通过显式知识工程解决这一缺陷，而不引入模型微调。

Method: DSR框架包含三个步骤：（1）Defactualize：用分层regex/NER/词法分类器流水线抽取显著实体，并在引导前以类型化占位符替换；（2）Steer：对去事实化后的表征施加激活引导以控制风格；（3）Rehydrate：生成后通过显著性引导的确定性恢复，将已验证的真实值还原。评估采用六个LLaMA系列模型（1B-13B）与专用的KG消融研究。

Result: 在600个A2A生成的客服案例（1200次生成）上，DSR相对纯引导基线显著提高了已验证实体的恢复率（Cohen's d=0.225, p_Bonf=1.0×10^-4），尽管绝对恢复率仍然适中；同时在不同模型家族上保持了有效的风格控制。逐层可分性与引导强度诊断还揭示了表征级引导与事实接地之间此前未探索的相互作用。

Conclusion: 显式知识工程能够系统地增强可信、可控且可复现的生成式AI，且无需模型微调；代码、缓存的引导向量与评估脚本已公开，以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) deployed in fact-sensitive applications such as customer support must simultaneously preserve factual correctness and generate responses in a controllable stylistic register. Activation steering enables fine-tuning-free style control by perturbing hidden representations, but it lacks an explicit mechanism for distinguishing verifiable facts from stylistic content, leading to semantic leakage. We address this challenge through \emph{Defactualize-Steer-Rehydrate} (DSR), a knowledge-engineering framework that integrates a typed, salience-weighted knowledge graph (KG) with activation steering. DSR extracts salient entities using a layered regex or NER or lexical-classifier pipeline, replaces them with typed placeholders prior to steering, and deterministically restores verified values through salience-guided rehydration after generation. DSR is evaluated across six LLaMA-family models (1B--13B parameters) on 600 A2A-generated customer-support cases (1,200 generations), with a dedicated KG ablation study. DSR significantly increases verified-entity recovery relative to a steering-only baseline (Cohen's $d=0.225$, $p_{\text{Bonf}}=1.0\times10^{-4}$), though the absolute recovery rate remains modest, while preserving effective style control across diverse model families. Layer-wise separability and steering-strength diagnostics further show previously unexplored interactions between representation-level steering and factual grounding. hese results demonstrate that explicit knowledge engineering can systematically enhance trustworthy, controllable, and reproducible generative AI without requiring model fine-tuning. Code, cached steering vectors, and evaluation scripts are publicly released to support reproducibility.\footnote{https://github.com/Tanmay-IITDSAI/KG-Gated-Defactualization}

</details>


### [115] [Self-Supervised Speech Representations Track Spoken Language Convergence to Adult Models in Infants and Children Who Are Deaf/Hard-of-Hearing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20396)
*L. Choy,A. S. Khan,S. Patrizi,D. Ye,J. Gross,M. Cychosz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展、语言中立的儿童语音发展评估方法：利用 HuBERT-BASE 语音嵌入直接从头戴式日常录音的声学信号中捕捉儿童语音向成人看护者模式趋同的过程。在对聋/重听儿童及其女性成年看护者超过 925 小时观察数据的分析中，嵌入距离随听觉年龄增长而减小（控制音高与发声时长后），并与多项标准化言语语言测量显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测量儿童语言发展（语音向成人模式趋同）依赖详细的转录和语言特异性专业知识，难以跨越不同语言和人群进行规模化评估。作者希望找到一种直接从声学信号出发、无需转录、语言无关且可扩展的客观指标。

Method: 使用 HuBERT-BASE 从长期儿童中心录音中提取语音嵌入；计算聋/重听儿童与其女性成人看护者嵌入之间的距离（$>$925 小时观测）；以听觉年龄为发展变量，控制音高和发声时长；将该单一距离指标与多个标准化言语语言测量进行关联验证。

Result: 嵌入距离随儿童听觉年龄增加而减小（在控制音高、发声时长后仍然成立），表明儿童语音模式随发展向看护者趋同；该单一距离指标与从婴儿期到学前期的多项标准化言语语言测量显著相关。

Conclusion: 研究指向一条从儿童日常生活的自然录音中实现可扩展、语言中立的言语发育评估的可行路径，有助于跨越语言和人群瓶颈，支持大规模监测与早期干预。

Abstract: Language development is characterized by a gradual convergence of children's speech toward adult patterns. Measuring this process has traditionally required detailed transcription and language-specific expertise, limiting scalability across languages and populations. Here, we use speech embeddings to capture this convergence directly from the acoustic signal in longform, child-centered recordings, taken as children go about their daily lives. Using HuBERT-BASE, we extracted embeddings from speech vocalizations of children who are deaf/hard-of-hearing and their female adult caregivers ($>$925 hrs. observation). Embedding distance between children and caregivers decreased with hearing age, controlling for pitch and vocalization length, indicating, as expected, that children's speech patterns converge to caregivers over development. This single distance metric likewise related to multiple standardized measures of speech and language from infancy through preschoolhood. These results suggest a path toward scalable, language-neutral assessment of spoken language development from children's everyday lives.

</details>


### [116] [LingShu: A Large-Scale Symptom-Centric Contextualized Knowledge Graph Bridging Traditional Chinese Medicine and Modern Biomedicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20402)
*Rui Hua,Zixin Shu,Kai Chang,Dengying Yan,Jianan Xia,Hui Zhu,Shujie Song,Shurui Yang,Tongxin Wang,Yue Yin,Yu Wei,Lijuan Pei,Yunhui Hu,Hao Xu,Mingzhong Xiao,Xiaodong Li,Haibin Yu,Runshun Zhang,Wenjia Wang,Baoyan Liu,Xuezhong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍LingShu，一个以症状为中心的大规模情境化生物医学知识图谱，旨在桥接中医与现代生物医学。该图谱包含1733万实体记录和3947万关系记录（1719万语义三元组、2229万情境化四元组），采用混合数据模型（64种三元关系模式+35种四元关系模式）显式编码条件性医学知识，并配套提供集成图可视化、图推理与循证问答代理的Web平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物医学知识图谱使用二元关系，难以表达生物医学知识中普遍存在的条件性/情境性特征。症状作为共享表型层，既能支撑中医的辨证论治（症状模式→证候→治法），又能连接现代生物医学的临床表现→疾病→分子机制，因此需要一个以症状为枢纽、能编码条件关系的大规模知识图谱来弥合两种医学体系。

Method: LingShu通过融合多源数据（临床电子病历、权威中医典籍、生物医学本体、策展知识库），采用自然语言处理、术语归一化与人在回路验证相结合的处理流程构建。其核心创新是混合数据模型：保留64种类型化三元关系模式以保证广泛连通性，同时引入35种情境化四元关系模式以捕获条件性医学关联（如证候依赖的方药疗效、疾病情境下的药物效应、人群特异性的临床关联、机制相关的治疗反应），从而显式编码关系发生的情境。

Result: 构建并分析了LingShu导出版本，规模达1733万原子级实体记录与3947万关系记录（1719万语义三元组、2229万情境化四元组），覆盖证候、症状、方药、疾病、靶点等核心类型；同时开发了web平台（http://www.tcmkg.com/），整合图可视化、图推理与基于证据的知识问答代理。

Conclusion: LingShu通过症状中心视角与情境化四元组数据模型，显式编码了中医—现代医学桥接中的条件性知识，为跨体系医学知识组织、关系推理与循证问答提供了细粒度的知识基础设施，证明了情境化知识图谱在复杂生物医学领域的可行性与实用价值。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) are pivotal for knowledge organization, yet traditional binary relations often struggle to represent the conditional nature of biomedical knowledge. Symptoms provide a shared phenotypic layer for linking Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM), which relies on symptom patterns for syndrome differentiation and treatment selection, with modern biomedicine, which connects clinical manifestations to diseases and molecular mechanisms. We present LingShu, a large-scale symptom-centric contextualized knowledge graph designed to bridge TCM and modern biomedicine. The exported version of LingShu analyzed in this study comprises 17.33 million atom-level entity records and 39.47 million relation records, including 17.19 million semantic triples and 22.29 million contextualized quadruples. LingShu integrates multi-source data, including clinical electronic medical records, authoritative TCM texts, biomedical ontologies, and curated knowledge bases, through a pipeline combining natural language processing, terminology normalization, and human-in-the-loop verification. A key innovation of LingShu is its hybrid data model: it maintains 64 typed triple relation patterns to ensure broad connectivity, while incorporating 35 contextual quadruple relation patterns to capture conditional medical associations. This dual-structure approach explicitly encodes conditional knowledge, providing a granular representation of the contexts associated with medical relations. These contextualized relations cover syndrome-dependent herb efficacy, disease-contextualized drug effects, population-specific clinical associations, and mechanism-related therapeutic responses. Furthermore, we developed a web platform (http://www.tcmkg.com/) that integrates graph visualization, graph-based reasoning, and an evidence-grounded knowledge question-answering agent.

</details>


### [117] [ARGUS: Theory-of-Mind Guided Argument Generation with Strategy-Aware Planning and Knowledge Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20405)
*Zhe Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Argus，一个基于智能体的说服性写作框架，通过 Theory-of-Mind 推理器显式建模受众信念与价值观，结合组件感知规划器（分配 logos、pathos、ethos、kairos 等修辞功能并触发策略引导的证据检索）与迭代优化模块，在三个基准上使用自动化 Elo 与 LLM 评委指标评估，持续优于强基线，并能在模拟实验中有效改变抗拒型受众的立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说服性论证生成需要建模受众信念、修辞策略与事实依据，但现有方法大多不区分受众（audience-agnostic），且未能将策略选择整合到生成过程中以提升说服力，因此存在明显的研究空白。

Method: Argus 的核心是一个 Theory-of-Mind（ToM）推理器，构建受众信念与价值观的显式双重心理模型以指导后续决策；该表征条件化一个组件感知规划器，将论证分解为子主题、分配细粒度修辞功能（logos、pathos、ethos、kairos），并在规划阶段触发策略引导的证据检索；最后通过一个优化模块迭代定位并解决多维弱点，且不造成质量回退。

Result: 在三个多样化基准上，采用自动化成对 Elo 与 LLM-as-judge 指标评估，Argus 在多种骨干模型上持续优于强基线，获得最高排名与最高总体得分；针对性的模拟实验进一步验证了其在改变抗拒型受众立场方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合受众心理建模与经典修辞策略的智能体框架能够显著提升说服性写作的质量与说服力，为受众感知的论证生成提供了有效的解决路径。

Abstract: Persuasive argument generation requires modeling audience beliefs, rhetorical strategies, and factual grounding. Despite recent advancements, existing methods remain largely audience-agnostic and fail to integrate strategy selection to improve persuasiveness. To bridge this gap, we propose Argus, an agent-based framework that operationalizes classical rhetoric for persuasive writing. At its core, a Theory-of-Mind (ToM) Reasoner constructs an explicit dual mental model of the audience's beliefs and values to guide downstream decisions. This representation conditions a component-aware planner that decomposes the argument into subtopics, assigns fine-grained rhetorical functions (logos, pathos, ethos, kairos), and triggers strategy-guided evidence retrieval at planning time. Finally, a refinement module iteratively targets and resolves multi-dimensional weaknesses without quality regression. We evaluate Argus across three diverse benchmarks using both automated pairwise Elo and LLM-as-judge metrics. Results show that Argus consistently outperforms strong baselines across multiple backbone models, achieving top rankings and the highest overall scores. Targeted simulation experiments further validate its effectiveness in shifting resistant audience stances.

</details>


### [118] [LiLiCorr: Lightweight Likelihood Correlation of Parallel Drafts for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20530)
*Matan Rusanovsky,Yoav Miron,Roy Uziel,Omer Belhasin,Ran Zilberstein,Maor Ashkenazi,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LiLiCorr 是一种轻量级的、基于似然的模型，用于在投机解码中关联草稿模型已生成的逐位置边缘分布。它保留每个位置的 top-k 候选 token 并联合处理，通过 in/out 向量的余弦相似度捕捉相邻候选之间的匹配关系，从而在不显式构造完整联合分布的情况下恢复块级联合一致性，显著提升接受长度与吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFlash 这类扩散式块头（block head）可以单次前向预测一整块未来 token，是很有吸引力的草稿器；但它基于逐位置边缘分布（per-position marginals）而非块联合分布训练，因此生成的 token 单独看合理，放在一起却缺乏连贯性（jointly incoherent）。现有工作在草稿阶段恢复连贯性的方法仍不够高效，需要一个轻量方案在草稿时恢复块级联合结构。

Method: LiLiCorr 的核心做法是：在草稿器已产生的逐位置边缘分布上，保留每个位置的 top-k token 作为候选，将它们联合处理，为每个候选生成一个 in 向量和一个 out 向量。相邻位置的候选对视为匹配，当较早位置的 out 向量与较后位置的 in 向量余弦相似度高时成立，这些匹配捕捉了块的联合结构而无需实体化完整联合分布。所有向量由一次轻量网络前向产生，配对分数作为批式矩阵运算并行计算，仅有廉价的贪心遍历是顺序的。此外，作者将草稿器与 LiLiCorr 协同训练，使草稿器学会提出能关联成更长接受序列的候选。

Result: 在全部基准上，LiLiCorr 相比原始 DFlash 草稿器将接受长度提升 9%–19%，其评分头仅约占每块延迟的 2.8%。与 DFlash 及两个同时期在草稿阶段恢复连贯性的方法相比，LiLiCorr 在 72 个设置中的 70 个取得最高吞吐量（涵盖九个基准、两种目标规模、贪心与 temperature-one 解码，以及六种并发、两种输入长度、三个熵层级的吞吐率扫描），所有系统在统一服务栈上同等优化。将 LiLiCorr 扩展到比训练长度高一个数量级的输入时，优势依然保持。

Conclusion: LiLiCorr 表明：通过在草稿阶段以轻量、并行的方式关联逐位置边缘分布，可以在不生成完整联合分布的前提下恢复块的联合连贯性，从而以极小的延迟开销换取显著的接受长度和吞吐量提升，并具有良好的长度外推能力，是投机解码中实用的块级草稿增强方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by drafting future tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. A diffusion-style block head such as DFlash is an attractive drafter, predicting an entire block of future tokens in one forward pass. However, it is trained on per-position marginals rather than the joint block distribution, so the tokens it emits are individually plausible yet jointly incoherent. We introduce LiLiCorr, a Lightweight Likelihood-based model that Correlates the per-position marginal distributions a drafter already produces. It keeps the top-k tokens at each position as candidates and processes them jointly, producing for each an in and an out vector. A pair of adjacent candidates matches when the earlier one's out vector has high cosine similarity with the later one's in vector. These matches capture the block's joint structure without ever materializing the full joint distribution. One lightweight network pass produces all the vectors, and the pairwise scores are then computed in parallel as batched matrix operations, leaving only a cheap greedy walk sequential. We further co-train the drafter with LiLiCorr, so it learns to propose candidates that correlate into longer accepted sequences. Over the vanilla DFlash drafter, LiLiCorr raises acceptance length on every benchmark by 9 to 19%, while its scoring head accounts for about 2.8% of the per-block latency. Against DFlash and two concurrent methods that also restore coherence at draft time, LiLiCorr delivers the highest throughput in 70 of 72 settings: nine benchmarks at two target sizes under greedy and temperature-one decoding, and a throughput sweep over six concurrencies, two input lengths and three entropy tiers, with all systems equally optimized on a common serving stack. Extending LiLiCorr to inputs an order of magnitude longer than it was trained on preserves that lead.

</details>


### [119] [JuryProbe: An Empirical Consensus-Risk Diagnostic for Routing Reference-Free Factuality Judge Panels to Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20607)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JuryProbe 是一个针对无参考（reference-free）事实性判断法官小组的共识风险实证诊断工具，并配套基于校准的路由策略。它通过假阴性-only 相关性（0.402/0.368）和假共识提升度（3.13x/18.13x）识别小组成员的共享盲区；当判定为高风险时，将无参考的多数接受路由到带可信参考的相同法官上。在审计的 FEVER 扰动中，路由策略在 34/34 个划分上与对所有多数接受进行接地的策略等价，固定预设规则能在 8-10/10 个划分上触发、在阴性对照中 0/10 触发，站立不动可避免 28% 的参考获取且仅增加 0.004 的假接受。该方法提供的是经验诊断而非形式化风险保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个廉价的 LLM 法官组成的小组越来越多地执行"接受或升级"决策。在事实性判断场景中，几个无参考法官的一致同意可能带来隐藏风险：表面的一致可能反映的是共享的假阴性盲区（共同盲点）而非独立的证据。因此，亟需一个能够诊断这种"假共识风险"的方法，以决定何时不应信任无参考小组的一致接受。

Method: JuryProbe 包含两个部分：(1) 经验共识风险诊断——从带标签的校准探针中估计仅假阴性（FN-only）的法官间相关性以及假共识提升度（false-consensus lift），以此量化小组成员错误之间的依赖；(2) 基于校准的路由策略——当诊断标记为高风险时，将无参考多数接受路由到同一组法官但配备可信参考（grounding）；否则正常接受。此外还评估了固定预设规则在多种数据族（合成、基准作者撰写、科学）与阴性对照上的表现，以及弱 BM25 检索下的鲁棒性和标签过期后的重新校准需求。

Result: 在审计的 FEVER 扰动上，无参考小组表现出相关的假阴性（FN-only 相关性 0.402 与 0.368；提升度 3.13x 与 18.13x），而一致假共识在可信参考的最佳情形诊断下对最小对与非最小对证据均降为零。路由策略在 34/34 个划分上验证为与"对所有无参考多数接受进行接地"等价，改进源于接受条件化的接地，而诊断决定是否激活。固定预设规则在合成、基准和科学族中标记 8-10/10 个划分，在阴性对照中 0/10；站立不动避免 28% 的参考获取，仅增加 0.004 的假接受。弱 BM25 检索下假接受减少依然保持，但覆盖代价显著；过期的站立不动标签需要周期性重新校准。

Conclusion: JuryProbe 不提供形式化风险保证，也未能在自然小组上建立可靠的站立不动决策；其支持的贡献是作为一种针对小组层面高错误依赖（高风险面板错误依赖）的经验诊断工具，用于触发将无参考接受转为带参考接地的路由策略，从而降低共享盲区导致的假接受风险。

Abstract: Panels of inexpensive LLM judges increasingly make accept-or-escalate decisions. In factuality settings, accepting a claim because several reference-free judges agree can create a hidden risk: agreement may reflect shared false-negative blind spots rather than independent evidence. We introduce JuryProbe, an empirical consensus-risk diagnostic for reference-free factuality judge panels, paired with a calibration-based routing policy. JuryProbe estimates consensus risk from a labeled calibration probe using false-negative-only (FN-only) judge correlation and false-consensus lift; when flagged high-risk, reference-free majority accepts are routed to the same judges with trusted references. On audited FEVER corruptions, reference-free panels show correlated false negatives (FN-only correlations 0.402 and 0.368; lifts 3.13x and 18.13x), while unanimous false consensus drops to zero under a trusted-reference best-case diagnostic on both minimal-pair and non-minimal-pair evidence. In flagged settings, the routed policy is by construction equivalent to grounding every reference-free majority accept (verified in 34/34 splits): improvement comes from accept-conditioned grounding, while the diagnostic determines whether to activate it. A fixed, pre-specified rule flags 8-10 of 10 splits across synthetic, benchmark-authored, and scientific families and 0 of 10 on a negative control, where standing down avoids 28% of reference acquisitions at a 0.004 increase in false accepts. False-accept reduction persists under weak BM25 retrieval at substantial coverage cost, while stale stand-down labels require periodic recalibration. JuryProbe provides no formal risk guarantee and does not establish reliable stand-down on natural panels; its supported contribution is an empirical diagnostic of high-risk panel error dependence.

</details>


### [120] [When Failures Propagate: Causal Failure Attribution in Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20627)
*Lauren Pothuru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgenticRAG-FP，一个用于 agentic RAG（检索增强生成）中因果性失败归因的干预式基准。它通过在指定跳（hop）注入已确认的故障，并重新执行下游轨迹，检验事后（post-hoc）追踪是否仍能识别被注入的跳。在 80 个三跳 MuSiQue 问题的完整扫描中，基于覆盖率的诊断在跳 1 达到 0.91，但在跳 2 和跳 3 均为 0.00；在内容篡改研究的深度 2（18 个失败案例）中，覆盖率诊断为 0.00，而冻结跳反事实探针为 0.67。结果将传播深度确立为诊断 agentic RAG 失败的一个显式评估轴，并区分了“事后信号丢失”的广泛证据与“小样本方法对比”的有限证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 agentic RAG 中，检索、推理与答案生成被交错在多个跳之间，某一跳（如跳 1）的检索错误可能直到跳 3 才表现为错误答案，而后续检索也可能修复轨迹。这使得“事后”识别失败根因变得困难。现有诊断缺乏可控的因果干预手段，因而无法确定诊断器是否真正归因到引发错误的跳。本文旨在提供一个带已知干预的基准，系统评估诊断器在轨迹后缀被改变后仍能识别注入故障跳的能力。

Method: AgenticRAG-FP 采用干预式设计：在指定跳注入一个经过认证（certified）的故障，随后重新执行下游轨迹，并将诊断器的归因结果与已知干预进行对比。核心评估问题是：当后缀（suffix）因干预而变化后，事后轨迹是否仍能识别被注入的跳。实验分为两部分：(1) 在 80 个三跳 MuSiQue 问题上对 Claude Haiku 4.5 完成严格密集扫描，给出每跳的失败轨迹数量（n=43/36/21）；(2) 内容篡改研究，在主题完整的证据中改动携带答案或桥梁信息的事实，并与覆盖率诊断、冻结跳反事实探针等诊断方法对比。

Result: 在三跳完整扫描中，基于覆盖率的诊断在跳 1 为 0.91，在跳 2 与跳 3 均为 0.00（n=43,36,21）。在内容篡改研究中，经筛选后深度 2 保留 18 个失败案例，其覆盖率诊断得分为 0.00，而冻结跳反事实探针的探索性合并对比得分为 0.67。深度 3 的内容估计因仅剩 3 个失败案例而仅作描述性呈现。

Conclusion: 上述结果将传播深度（propagation depth）确立为诊断 agentic RAG 失败的显式评估轴。诊断随失败发生的深度而显著退化（浅层跳可被识别，深层跳几乎无法通过事后覆盖率信号识别），而冻结跳反事实探针在深层（深度 2）表现出更好的归因能力。作者强调应区分两类证据：一类是表明“事后信号在传播中丢失”的广泛、稳健的证据；另一类则是基于小样本的方法间对比（如深度 2 的 18 例）与描述性估计（深度 3 仅 3 例），后者需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) interleaves retrieval, reasoning, and answer generation across multiple hops. A retrieval error at hop 1 can surface only as a wrong answer at hop 3, while later retrieval can also repair the trajectory. This paper introduces AgenticRAG-FP, an interventional benchmark for causal failure attribution in agentic RAG. The benchmark injects a certified fault at a specified hop, re-executes the downstream trajectory, and evaluates diagnosers against the known intervention. Its central question is whether a post-hoc trace still identifies the injected hop after the suffix changes. In the completed strict dense Claude Haiku 4.5 sweep on 80 three-hop MuSiQue questions, coverage-based diagnosis is 0.91 at hop 1 and 0.00 at hops 2 and 3 (n=43,36,21 failed trajectories). A smaller content-corruption study changes an answer-bearing or bridge fact in topically intact evidence. At depth 2, where 18 failed cases remain after filtering, coverage-based diagnosis is 0.00 and a frozen-hop counterfactual probe is 0.67 in an exploratory pooled comparison. Depth-3 content estimates are descriptive only because they contain three failed cases. These results make propagation depth an explicit evaluation axis for diagnosing agentic RAG failures while distinguishing broad evidence of post-hoc signal loss from small-sample method comparisons.

</details>


### [121] [Sparse Token Routing in Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20632)
*Sai Krishna Arthanari,JaeHyeong Chang,Chengzhe Sun,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对“并非所有token都需要相同计算量”这一高效Transformer核心假设进行了端到端验证。作者提出SEWN——一种双流Transformer架构，通过学习得到的门控机制将token路由到轻量或全容量处理路径。实验发现：与参数匹配的基线相比，路由仅带来可忽略的精度变化；门控的token重要性信号高度依赖于其学习方式——静态词表先验在BoolQ上的反事实忠实性测试中失败，而全上下文门控在两个任务上都实现了高度显著的分离（p<10^-10），且不改变任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效Transformer研究（如token剪枝、自适应计算）通常以“并非所有token都需要同等的计算投入”作为出发点。然而这一假设本身缺乏严格的端到端检验——既有方法的加速收益与token重要性信号的有效性之间的因果关系尚未被系统验证。作者旨在直接测试这一核心前提是否成立，以及token重要性的判定方式（先验vs.上下文）如何影响路由效果。

Method: 提出SEWN（双流Transformer）：每个token由一个学习得到的门控决策其经过轻量处理路径还是全容量处理路径，从而将自适应计算与token重要性判定分离。实验设计了参数匹配的基线以公平比较精度；同时构造了反事实忠实性测试（counterfactual faithfulness test，应用于BoolQ）来检验门控给出的token重要性信号是否真实反映语义贡献。对比对象包括静态词表种子先验门控与全上下文门控。

Result: (1) 与参数匹配基线相比，路由引入的精度变化可忽略不计，说明轻量路径并未显著损害任务性能；(2) 门控的token重要性信号的可信度完全取决于其训练方式：静态词表种子的先验门控在BoolQ反事实测试中失败（无法忠实识别关键token），而全上下文门控在两个任务上均达到高度显著的分离（p<10^-10）；(3) 全上下文门控取得显著重要性分离的同时，任务精度不变，表明重要性信号与端到端精度是相互独立的现象。

Conclusion: 高效Transformer的核心假设（不同token需要不同计算量）部分成立但需要重要限定：路由确实能以几乎不损失精度为代价实现计算分配，但若据此声称门控“识别了重要token”，则必须依赖全上下文学习方式——静态先验无法通过反事实忠实性检验。因此，token路由研究应将“计算效率”与“重要性信号的可解释性/忠实性”作为两个可分离的维度分别评估。

Abstract: Efficient-transformer research often motivates token pruning and adaptive computation with the claim that not all tokens require equal computational effort. We test this claim end to end using SEWN, a two-stream Transformer that routes tokens through either lightweight or full-capacity processing using a learned gate. Across our experiments, routing introduces negligible accuracy change relative to parameter-matched baselines, while the gate's token-importance signal depends critically on how it is learned. A static lexicon-seeded prior fails a counterfactual faithfulness test on BoolQ, whereas a fully contextual gate achieves highly significant separation ($p<10^{-10}$) on both evaluated tasks without changing task accuracy.

</details>


### [122] [AgentMercury: Your Agent Can Synthesize Verifiable Environments for Business Scenarios at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20634)
*Minbyul Jeong,Chanwoong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentMercury是一个可扩展的环境合成框架，从高层业务场景自动生成可执行环境（持久化世界：实体、服务、工具、状态及跨服务不变量），从中自然涌现多样化任务。共构建4,783个覆盖14个行业、50个国家的可执行环境，作为强化学习训练基质后，即使未针对评测基准生成，训练得到的策略在企业管理流程及推理、编码、科学计算、工具使用等域外基准上均显著提升（如Qwen3.5-4B在EnterpriseOps-GYM上从12.3升至15.7，在AIME26上从45.9升至56.0）。此外，环境构建过程本身可被学习：对Qwen3.5-35B-A3B用构建轨迹微调后，在保留业务场景上的可执行世界创作成功率从3.3%提升至83.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习的训练环境多为手工构建或围绕预定义任务/基准合成，这种任务中心范式难以扩展到能反映真实、演化中的工作流的环境——在现实世界中，多样化任务是自然涌现的。作者旨在突破"为特定任务构建环境"的限制，转向"由世界驱动任务涌现"的范 paradigm（场景导向、世界优先），从而获得更通用、可扩展的学习信号。

Method: 核心方法是两阶段合成流程：1) 环境合成（世界构建）：从高层业务场景出发，先实例化一个持久化世界，包含实体、服务、工具、状态和可执行的跨服务不变量，使多样任务与交互轨迹从中涌现，而非直接构建特定任务。2) 学习利用：将4,783个环境作为强化学习训练基质训练策略；同时把"构建过程"本身建模为可学习能力，通过构建轨迹微调生成模型以提升可执行世界的创作成功率。

Result: 跨14个行业、50个国家构建4,783个可执行环境。训练出的策略在企业管理工作流与域外基准上均显著提升：Qwen3.5-4B在EnterpriseOps-GYM上从12.3提升至15.7，在AIME26上从45.9提升至56.0。环境构建过程可学习：用构建轨迹微调Qwen3.5-35B-A3B后，保留业务场景上的可执行世界创作成功率从3.3%升至83.3%。

Conclusion: 场景导向（scenario-grounded）的环境能够提供超越基准特定训练的有用且可泛化的学习信号；同时，可执行世界的构建本身可以成为一种可学习的能力。这一结果表明"世界先于任务"的环境合成范式有望成为可扩展、通用训练基质的有效途径。

Abstract: Agents learn to act through interaction with environments, yet the environments used for training are often manually constructed or synthesized around predefined tasks and benchmarks. This task-centric paradigm makes it difficult to scale environments that reflect realistic and evolving workflows where diverse tasks can naturally emerge from the underlying world. We introduce AgentMercury, a scalable framework for synthesizing executable environments from high-level business scenarios. Rather than constructing an environment for a specific task, AgentMercury first instantiates a persistent world with entities, services, tools, state, and executable cross-service invariants, from which diverse tasks and interaction trajectories can subsequently emerge. We construct 4,783 executable environments spanning 14 industries and 50 countries, and use them as training substrates for reinforcement learning. Despite being generated without targeting the evaluation benchmarks, policies trained on these business-oriented environments improve substantially on both enterprise workflows and out-of-domain benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, scientific computing, and tool use. In our experiments, Qwen3.5-4B improves from 12.3 to 15.7 on EnterpriseOps-GYM and from 45.9 to 56.0 on AIME26 after training on AgentMercury environments. We further show that the construction process itself can be learned: fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on construction traces increases executable-world authoring success from 3.3% to 83.3% on held-out business scenarios. These results show that scenario-grounded environments can provide useful and generalizable learning signals beyond benchmark-specific training, while their construction can itself become a learnable capability.

</details>


### [123] [MIL-BERT: Classification of Arbitrarily Large Text with Performance and Explanatory Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20636)
*John Cadigan,Dayne Freitag,Eric Yeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多实例学习（MIL）的神经网络文本分类算法，该算法不是对整个长文本进行分类，而是从文本中选取关键片段（excerpts）进行决策。该方法可扩展到近100万token的样本，在7个数据集上评估（重点是远超基础模型编码限制的长文本集合），并在3个数据集上达到SOTA：新闻媒体政治偏见识别、长故事内容警告、推文作者人口统计学特征。此外，在弱标注文本包（bags）上训练的模型还能泛化到更小的实例上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多文本分类决策仅基于文本中的部分片段即可完成，而现有神经网络模型受编码长度限制难以处理超长文本。作者借鉴多实例学习的思想，希望训练一个神经网络能够自动挑选关键片段进行分类，从而解决长文本分类问题。

Method: 采用多实例学习框架：将文本视为包（bag），通过神经网络的注意力/选择机制挑选具有判别力的关键片段，仅基于这些片段而非全文做出分类决策。该算法通过端到端训练学习片段选择，且具有可扩展性，能在近100万token的样本上进行训练。

Result: 在7个数据集上评估，其中3个数据集取得SOTA：新闻媒体政治偏见识别、长故事触发警告、推文作者人口统计特征。弱标注包（bag）上训练的模型可泛化到准确分类更小的实例；该方法是为数不多能在这些长文本数据集上表现出色的神经方法之一。

Conclusion: 基于片段选择的多实例学习是一种有效且可扩展的长文本分类方案，它超越了基础模型编码限制，在多个长文任务上取得了当前最优性能，并展示了从弱标签包到实例级别的泛化能力。

Abstract: Many text classification decisions are viable based on constituent excerpts alone. Taking inspiration from the field of multiple instance learning, we present an algorithm for training a neural network to classify text by selecting such excerpts. We show that our approach is also scalable with demonstrated learning against samples with nearly 1M tokens. We evaluate our methods on 7 datasets with emphasis on long-textual collections that far exceed the encoding limit of our base model. We present state-of-the-art results with this algorithm on 3 datasets: identification of political bias in news outlets, trigger warnings in long stories, and demographic characteristics of authors in tweet collections. Furthermore, the model trained on weakly-labeled collections of text (bags) generalizes to accurately classify constituent, smaller instances. Besides a new state-of-the-art for these problems, this approach is one of the few neural methods to excel in these datasets.

</details>


### [124] [Directional Contextual Representations for Dependency Relations: Why Cross-Direction Pairing Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20647)
*Sai Krishna Arthanari,JaeHyeong Chang,Chengzhe Sun,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将双向LSTM的上下文表示拆分为前向-only（F_i）和后向-only（B_i）两个部分。对于依存关系类型分类任务，两者单独使用均优于融合的自注意力表示，但"交叉方向"配对（F_i vs B_j）始终不如"同方向"配对（F_i vs B_j 中的同向），且性能差距随token距离增大而扩大。通过冻结主干（frozen-trunk）诊断方法，作者排除了架构信息泄漏和训练协同适应（co-adaptation）作为主因，并发现部分表征冗余与预期性编码虽是真实效应，但无法完整解释差距；进一步诊断表明方向信息确实存在但未被精确定位，且仅传播少数token后即衰减，这与距离增长现象在机制上一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双向LSTM中前向/后向隐藏状态是否包含互补的、可拆分的方向性信息，并解释为何"交叉方向"配对（用候选token的后向状态匹配当前token的前向状态）在依存关系类型分类中系统性劣于"同方向"配对，且惩罚随距离增大——这涉及方向性信息的存储、传播与定位机制，对理解表征构成与模型诊断有重要意义。

Method: 采用多层次的诊断与消融方法：(1) 使用单层BiLSTM并通过代码检查确保架构层无方向间信息泄漏；(2) 冻结主干仅训练新头部（frozen-trunk），检验训练协同适应的影响；(3) 线性回归分析F_i与B_i的表征冗余度（对照打乱数据）；(4) 线性探针检测F_i对后续token的预期性编码；(5) 扩展的诊断：位置探针（positional probe）与距离衰减探针（distance-decay probe）检验方向信息的定位与传播范围；(6) 辅以paired-bootstrap显著性检验。

Result: F_i与B_i单独均优于融合的自注意力表征；交叉方向配对一致性地逊于同方向配对，且随token距离增加惩罚增大（paired-bootstrap显著）。冻结主干后93%的同向-交叉差距仍然存在，排除训练协同适应；线性回归显示F_i与B_i存在部分冗余（R²=0.324 vs 打乱对照0.028），线性探针显示F_i部分预期编码后续token（36.5% vs 17.2%多数基线），但二者（单独或组合）均不能完全解释全部差距。位置探针与距离衰减探针显示：方向信息被真实存储但未精确定位，仅传播少数token后衰减至基线水平，与"距离越远惩罚越大"的发现机制性对应。

Conclusion: 拆分双向表示（同方向配对）优于融合表示，而交叉方向配对的结构性劣势源于方向信息在空间上的模糊性（未精确定位）与短程传播特性，而非架构泄漏或训练协同适应。这一机制性解释为改进双向序列模型的表征设计提供了方向：若需利用长距离的跨方向匹配，需显式强化方向信息的位置编码与传播距离。

Abstract: Splitting a bidirectional LSTM's contextual representation into a forward-only $F_i$ (strictly a function of tokens $1..i$) and a backward-only $B_i$ (strictly a function of tokens $i..n$) beats either alone and beats a fused self-attention representation for dependency relation-type classification. But a specific, natural extension of this idea -- pairing a token's forward state against a \emph{candidate}'s backward state (``cross-direction'' pairing, $F_i$ vs.\ $B_j$) -- consistently \emph{underperforms} same-direction pairing, and the penalty \emph{grows}, not shrinks, with token distance, both paired-bootstrap significant. We diagnose why using a frozen-trunk methodology: architectural information leakage between directions is impossible by construction (a single-layer BiLSTM, verified by code inspection); 93\% of the same-vs-cross gap survives freezing the trunk and training only fresh heads, ruling out training-co-adaptation as the primary cause; linear regression shows partial representational redundancy between $F_i$ and $B_i$ ($R^2{=}0.324$ vs.\ $0.028$ for a shuffled control) and a linear probe shows partial anticipatory encoding of upcoming tokens in $F_i$ (36.5\% vs.\ 17.2\% majority baseline) -- real effects, but neither alone, nor combined, cleanly explains the full gap. Extended frozen-trunk diagnostics (a positional probe and a distance-decay probe) show directional information is genuinely stored but not exactly positioned, and propagates only a few tokens before decaying to baseline -- consistent with, and mechanistically underneath, the distance-growth finding.

</details>


### [125] [AsmEvo: Agentic Assembly-Level Optimization of AMD GPU Kernels with Functional Equivalence Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20711)
*Ji Liu,Puyuan Yang,Rongzhang Zheng,Fan Wang,Jinglin Wang,Muhammad A. Awad,Mortis Huang,Andy Chang,Zekai Li,Zeping Li,Zihao An,Yue Liu,Yuchen Yang,Jianghui Wang,Chushi Chen,Ziqiong Liu,Fuwei Yang,Dong Li,Wen Heng Chung,Shengcai Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AsmEvo，一个面向 AMD GPU 已编译代码对象（code object）的代理式汇编级优化器。它直接以已部署二进制为唯一行为 oracle，通过代码对象恢复、元数据感知重建、长时程代理的低级编辑、ABI 保持重建、差分验证、正确性门控计时与保守的就地补丁回退，对 AMDGPU 汇编进行优化，在不改变功能等价性的前提下显著提升内核性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能 ML 系统严重依赖 GPU 内核，但这些内核的可编辑源码往往不可用、由机器生成，或距离最终机器码太远而无法暴露剩余优化空间。现有 LLM 内核优化器与自动调优器主要面向 CUDA、Triton、HIP 或张量程序源码，并对照参考实现验证。作者研究更严格的场景：优化已编译的 AMDGPU 代码对象，此时部署的二进制是唯一可用的行为 oracle，缺乏源码与参考实现。

Method: 给定 AMDGPU 代码对象 K0，AsmEvo 执行五步流水线：(1) 重构可重汇编（reassemblable）的表示；(2) 由长时程（long-horizon）代理在汇编层面提出低级别的编辑；(3) 重建保持 ABI 的优化对象；(4) 仅在相同启动条件下与 K0 进行差分验证后才接受候选；(5) 结合代码对象恢复、元数据感知重建、剖析引导的热窗口编辑、正确性门控计时以及保守的就地补丁回退策略。

Result: 在 MI308X 上，AsmEvo 改进了 30 个选定的 KernelBench 内核中的 29 个，达到 1.35x 几何平均加速与 3.88x 最大加速。在 MI300X 生产负载上，它改进了所有评估的 AITer 二进制与 vLLM/SGLang Triton 汇编内核，分别达到 1.09x/1.31x 与 1.18x/1.34x 的几何平均/最大加速，同时保持功能等价性。

Conclusion: AsmEvo 证明，在没有源码和参考实现、仅凭已编译二进制作为行为 oracle 的严格设定下，基于代理的汇编级优化依然能够取得一致且显著的加速；二进制层面的优化可作为现有源码级优化器与自动调优器的有效补充，适用于内核源码不可用或被生成工具封装的生产场景。

Abstract: High-performance ML systems increasingly rely on GPU kernels whose editable source is unavailable, generated, or too distant from final machine code to expose remaining optimizations. Existing LLM kernel optimizers and autotuners mainly operate on CUDA, Triton, HIP, or tensor-program source and validate against reference implementations. We study a stricter setting: optimizing an already compiled AMDGPU code object, where the deployed binary is the only behavioral oracle.
