Result: 在多样化的联邦图学习任务上的实证结果表明，FlatLand 相较于现有方法取得了更优的性能，尤其在低维嵌入设置下优势明显。

Conclusion: FlatLand 验证了双曲几何（洛伦兹空间）作为图联邦学习个性化建模框架的有效性，证明类时/类空参数解耦能够以简洁的方式同时处理图结构负曲率与客户端异质性，为后续图 PFL 研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but highly heterogeneous client data remain challenging, especially in graph federated learning where clients possess structurally diverse graphs. Existing personalized federated learning (PFL) methods ignore the intrinsic geometric properties of diverse graph structures. We propose FlatLand, a novel personalized federated learning method that embeds different clients' data in tailored Lorentz space of hyperbolic geometry. Our key insight is that hyperbolic geometry naturally accommodates the intrinsic negative curvature prevalent in real-world graphs, while the time-like dimension in Lorentz space provides a principled way to encode client-specific heterogeneity. We develop a parameter decoupling strategy that separates heterogeneous information (captured in time-like parameters) from common knowledge (preserved in space-like parameters), enabling direct aggregation without requiring client similarity estimation and extra calculation modules. Empirical results on diverse federated graph learning tasks demonstrate that FlatLand achieves superior performance, particularly in low-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [282] [BackDFL: A Unified Benchmark For Backdoor Attacks and Defenses In Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21137)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Gregory Blanc,Yufei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BackDFL，一个用于系统评估去中心化联邦学习（DFL）在后门攻击下鲁棒性的统一基准。实验表明，现有最先进的 Byzantine 鲁棒 DFL 方法及适配的 FL 后门防御，在低至 15% 的恶意参与率下即告失效，尤其在异质性场景中，且其鲁棒性随通信图拓扑显著变化，说明 DFL 的安全性被严重高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFL 用点对点模型交换取代中心化参数服务器，从根本上重塑了威胁模型。缺乏全局协调聚合使其尤其易受后门攻击，但现有研究依赖简化的威胁模型、非自适应攻击者、碎片化的评估协议、不一致的通信拓扑和临时训练配置，导致对 DFL 安全性的理解不完整，其鲁棒性被显著高估。

Method: 作者构建了 BackDFL，一个统一的基准框架，用于在现实且自适应的后门攻击下系统评估 DFL。通过大规模实验，在一致性配置下对比多种防御方法，覆盖不同通信图拓扑与数据异质性设置。

Result: BackDFL 揭示了去中心化学习的严重失败模式：最先进的 Byzantine 鲁棒 DFL 方法和经过适配的 FL 后门防御在低至 15% 的恶意参与率下即失效（尤其异质性场景），且防御鲁棒性在不同通信图拓扑间差异显著。

Conclusion: 现有 DFL 防御的安全性被高估；要实现真正可信的 DFL，需要更现实的威胁模型、自适应攻击评估和统一基准，BackDFL 为此提供了基础。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) promises trust-free collaborative learning by replacing the centralized parameter server with peer-to-peer model exchange. However, this architectural shift fundamentally reshapes the threat landscape. Without globally coordinated aggregation, DFL becomes particularly susceptible to backdoor attacks, in which malicious participants implant persistent hidden behaviors while maintaining high clean-task performance. In this paper, we argue that the robustness of DFL has been significantly overestimated. Existing studies rely on simplified threat models, non-adaptive adversaries, fragmented evaluation protocols, inconsistent communication topologies, and ad hoc training configurations, leading to an incomplete understanding of DFL security. To address these limitations, we present BackDFL, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating DFL under realistic and adaptive backdoor attacks. Through extensive experiments, BackDFL exposes critical failure modes of decentralized learning. Our results demonstrate that both state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust DFL methods and adapted FL backdoor defenses fail under modest malicious participation rates (as low as 15%), especially in heterogeneous settings, while their robustness varies substantially across communication graph topologies.

</details>


### [283] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COEC是一个无需训练的LLM结构化剪枝补偿框架，通过对保留权重施加交替的左、右正交旋转，并在缩减Stiefel流形上优化右旋转，同时用广义交叉验证重标定奇异值、缓和校准Gram矩阵并引入对齐惩罚。它仅需小校准集的二阶统计量，不依赖剪枝准则，在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5系列多个稀疏度上显著改善困惑度与零样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝移除权重列会造成输出误差并损害模型精度。现有无需训练的补偿方法（如加性偏置或单一正交旋转）只调整保留权重的输出侧，其输入奇异框架保持不变，限制了保留权重在删列后自适应调整的能力，需要更灵活的补偿机制来恢复性能。

Method: 提出COEC框架：对保留权重交替施加左右正交旋转；右旋转在缩减Stiefel流形上优化；通过广义交叉验证逐层选择正则强度来重标定奇异值；对校准Gram矩阵进行温度化（tempering）以降低高能量激活方向的主导性；引入对齐惩罚保持相邻注意力投影间的几何关系。所有组件仅使用小规模校准集的二阶统计量，无需对LLM反向传播或重训练，且与剪枝准则无关。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5模型家族、多种结构化稀疏度下，COEC在全部模型上改善困惑度，并在大多数设置中提升零样本准确率，超越现有补偿方法；在更高稀疏度下收益更大。

Conclusion: 结果表明，剪枝后的补偿能够在无需训练的前提下部分恢复因删列而损失的模型性能，COEC可作为与剪枝准则正交的通用补偿模块，提升结构化剪枝LLM的有效性。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>


### [284] [Capturing Cardiac Cyclicity through Phase-Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21147)
*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Winder，一种利用心动周期固有循环结构的联合嵌入自监督表征学习架构。它通过相位等变自监督目标和由周期几何推导的固定闭式传输算子，将表征组织为相位不变坐标与相位旋转谐波子空间。在 PTB-XL 上以冻结线性探针协议评估，Winder 以约 1M 参数达到与 SOTA 自监督方法相当的诊断准确率，并展现出相位等变的潜在几何结构，证明了显式编码心脏相位对称性可保留诊断信息且具有参数高效、可解读的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理过程的循环结构可作为自监督表征学习的天然先验，而心动周期是这一先验最明确的应用场景。作者旨在利用心脏周期固有的相位对称性，使学习到的表征不仅保留诊断信息，还具备可解读、参数高效的潜在几何结构，并直接关联到可测量的生理量（心脏相位）。

Method: 1) 推导相位等变自监督目标；2) 提出 Winder 联合嵌入架构，将表征分解为相位不变坐标和相位旋转的谐波子空间；3) 传输算子为固定且闭式形式，由周期几何直接推导而非学习获得，不增加任何参数；4) 在 PTB-XL 数据集上采用冻结线性探针协议进行评估。

Result: 在 PTB-XL 上，Winder 以约 1M 参数的自监督模型规模，在冻结线性探针协议下取得了与现有 SOTA 自监督方法报道范围相当的诊断准确率，同时表现出相位等变的潜在几何结构。

Conclusion: 显式编码心脏相位对称性能够在保留诊断有用信息的同时，产生可解读、参数高效且与可测量生理量直接对应的潜在几何结构，验证了将周期结构作为自监督学习先验的价值。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [285] [Thermo-FL: Thermal-Aware Robust Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21172)
*Shiva Shrestha,Kazi Shaharair Sharif,Zongxing Xie,Jiajing Huang,Anhao Xiang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Thermo-FL，一个热感知的联邦 LoRA 微调框架，将设备温度作为主动控制信号，在客户端动态调节激活的 LoRA 层比例与稀疏更新传输密度以缓解热降频，并在服务端引入 TERRA 鲁棒聚合流水线（包含范数过滤、掩码感知方向校验、自适应活动坐标裁剪与掩码感知聚合），同时应对硬件不稳定和拜占庭/通信层恶意更新。实验在大型仿真器与 Jetson 物理测试床上验证其鲁棒性、温度稳定性、上传压缩效益与下游任务效用保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调可在不集中私有数据的情况下让大语言模型适配边缘设备，但实际部署必须同时应对两类挑战：受热约束的客户端可能降频、拖慢本地训练或延迟同步聚合；拜占庭客户端及通信层攻击者可能破坏用于形成全局模型的更新。现有方法未能联合处理硬件热不稳定与对抗性更新破坏。

Method: Thermo-FL 采用双端协同设计：客户端侧以设备温度作为主动控制信号，随设备升温/降温动态调整激活的 LoRA 层比例与传输的更新密度，在热应力下降低计算与传输负载；服务端侧提出 TERRA 鲁棒聚合流水线，针对动态稀疏的 LoRA 更新执行范数过滤、掩码感知的方向校验、自适应活动坐标裁剪和掩码感知聚合。

Result: 在大型仿真器中，Thermo-FL 在对抗性稀疏聚合场景下提升鲁棒性，在干净与攻击设置下均获得最优 BoolQ 精度，并在 GSM8K 上保持竞争力。在 Jetson 物理测试床上，Thermo-FL 稳定设备温度，通过位图稀疏编码减小压缩上传体积，并在符号翻转/缩放和 MITM 扰动下保持 GSM8K 效用。

Conclusion: 结果表明，安全的边缘大模型自适应应联合考虑硬件行为、负载调节、稀疏通信与聚合鲁棒性，Thermo-FL 为这一多维度问题提供了可行的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables large language models to adapt on edge devices without centralizing private data, but practical deployments must address hardware instability and adversarial update corruption together. Thermally constrained clients may throttle, slow local training, or delay synchronous aggregation, while Byzantine clients and communication-layer adversaries can corrupt the updates used to form the global model. To address these challenges, we present Thermo-FL, a thermal-aware federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that uses device temperature as an active control signal for local adapter training and sparse update transmission. On the client side, Thermo-FL adjusts the active LoRA-layer fraction and transmitted update density as devices heat or cool, reducing workload under thermal stress. On the server side, Thermo-FL introduces TERRA, a robust aggregation pipeline for dynamically sparse LoRA updates that combines norm filtering, mask-aware directional validation, adaptive active-coordinate clipping, and mask-aware aggregation. We evaluate Thermo-FL using both a large-scale emulator and a Jetson-based physical testbed. In the emulator, Thermo-FL improves robustness under adversarial sparse aggregation and achieves the strongest BoolQ accuracy across clean and attack settings while remaining competitive on GSM8K. In the physical prototype, Thermo-FL stabilizes device temperature, reduces compressed upload size through bitmap sparse encoding, and preserves GSM8K utility under sign-flip/scale and MITM perturbations. These results show that secure edge LLM adaptation should jointly consider hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and aggregation robustness.

</details>


### [286] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NSPIN 是一个神经符号框架，用于从非结构化临床叙述中归纳概率规划领域模型。它利用预训练 LLM 从原始文本中提取并填补结构化事件序列，归纳 PPDDL 模型，并通过 LLM 提出的修订方案结合经验验证来优化前置条件。在 9 名外科医生撰写的 2,660 份腹腔镜阑尾切除术记录上评估，生成的模型能泛化到未见过的记录，专家临床审查表明其归纳的知识与外科实践高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜阑尾切除术等外科手术流程复杂且高风险，但其工作流的正式化（用于决策支持）仍是一大挑战。由于缺乏结构化事件数据，且临床叙述中普遍存在隐式动作，经验性符号方法和 LLM 都无法单独充分解决在该场景下归纳概率规划领域模型的问题。

Method: 采用神经符号混合策略：先用预训练 LLM 从原始文本中提取并填补结构化事件序列；然后归纳 PPDDL 概率规划领域模型；最后用 LLM 提出的前置条件修订方案进行优化，并以经验验证作为指导。

Result: 在 9 名外科医生撰写的 2,660 份腹腔镜阑尾切除术手术记录上评估，NSPIN 生成的模型能够泛化到未见过的记录；专家临床审查表明其归纳出的知识在很大程度上与外科实践一致。

Conclusion: NSPIN 表明，将 LLM 的事件提取/修订能力与符号模型归纳及经验验证相结合，能够从非结构化临床叙述中可靠地构建概率规划领域模型，为外科工作流决策支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [287] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tydra，一种将 Transformer 与状态空间模型（SSM）层交错混合的表格数据上下文学习架构。在 30 个 OpenML 数据集上，相较于 TabPFN，Tydra 将推理时间减少约 30%，同时保留了大部分预测性能，且推理速度快于一个体积约大十倍（参数规模更大）的 Hydra 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Transformer 的表格基础模型（如 TabPFN）预测性能强，但其自注意力机制随上下文长度呈二次方计算开销；而基于 SSM 的次二次方替代方案（如 Hydra）虽高效却牺牲了精度。作者希望在效率与准确性之间取得平衡，因此探索混合架构。

Method: Tydra 采用混合架构设计，交替堆叠注意力层与 SSM 层，用于表格数据的上下文学习（in-context learning）。通过这种交错结构，结合 Transformer 的强表征能力与 SSM 的亚二次方计算复杂度，在保证预测性能的同时降低计算成本。

Result: 在 30 个 OpenML 数据集上的实验表明：Tydra 相比 TabPFN 推理时间降低约 30%，且预测性能损失很小；同时 Tydra 在性能上优于参数规模约为其十倍（推理也更慢）的 Hydra 模型，并提供更快的推理。

Conclusion: 结果表明混合架构是表格基础模型一个有前景的研究方向，能够在预测性能与推理效率之间实现良好的权衡。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [288] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAIR（Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine）框架用于生理时间序列（如动脉血压、血糖）的缺失值插补。其核心思想是先学习一条粗糙的基线曲线，再通过Transformer精修器进行多轮迭代修正，使其逼近生理真实。CAIR在连续血糖监测（AI-READI）和ICU动脉血压（MIMIC-III）上，按缺失长度与缺失机制（MCAR/MAR/NMAR）分层评估均取得最优精度，且在难度越高时优势越大（相对最强基线从MCAR下的9%扩大到依赖值缺失下的19%），并同时兼顾临床负荷指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据的缺失普遍存在，插补至关重要，但现代学习方法在此领域表现不佳，简单线性插值甚至胜过所有学习型插补器。作者指出根因在于生理缺失的两个特性被通用插补器忽略：缺失可能发生在临床极端（而非典型）信号处；缺失长度差距可达数量级。此外，仅低重构误差并不能恢复临床医生实际依赖的负荷指标。

Method: CAIR为两阶段框架：(1) 双向GRU插补器生成粗略基线曲线；(2) Transformer精修器连续三次迭代修正自身估计，向生理真实靠拢；两者在信号无关的随机缺口课程（random-gap curriculum）下联合训练。

Result: 在AI-READI（连续血糖监测）和MIMIC-III（ICU动脉血压）上，CAIR在每种缺失机制下均最准确；对最强基线优势随难度增大，从MCAR下的9%扩大到依赖值缺失（NMAR）下的19%。且CAIR在重构误差与临床负荷指标两条轴上都位居前列，而仅误差匹配的插补器无法保持负荷指标，能恢复负荷指标的插补器则精度远逊。

Conclusion: 生理时间序列插补需要针对其特殊缺失模式设计：应捕捉极端值缺失与跨数量级的缺口长度，采用"先插值、后迭代精修"的策略而非单次预测；同时评估应区分缺失长度与机制，并兼顾临床负担指标，避免单一平均指标掩盖真实性能差异。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [289] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C 是一种面向对齐多流遥测数据的可审计、严格先验、秩校准异常检测器，基于同一工况滚动中位数/MAD残差构建三个窗口通道（最大归一化局部和、鲁棒z残差上的高斯copula形式依赖对比、最差标准化AR(1)创新），将通道秩经Fisher聚合后再与历史聚合秩排序。在2019年英国电网测试中，TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah排在首位，但消融实验表明该排名来自局部通道而非copula通道；2020年冻结窗口无任何窗口入选，归因于记录规则饱和而非无事件年份。论文同时声明三项解释性限制：p值是选择量而非事件概率、copula形式通道并非字面意义的copula密度、经验秩计数只是诊断而非覆盖率或FDR证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多流遥测数据的单流监控存在盲区：单个数据流各自的取值区间看似正常时，多个流的联合行为可能共同异常。传统逐流阈值检测器无法捕捉这种跨流联合异常，因此需要一个能对多流联合状态进行整体评分与可审计排序的检测框架。

Method: TRACE-C 采用严格先验（frozen）的秩校准流程：以同一运行工况下的滚动中位数/MAD残差为输入，构造三类窗口通道——(1) 最大归一化局部和（local）捕捉幅值聚集；(2) 对鲁棒z残差施加高斯copula形式的依赖对比通道（copula-form），刻画跨流相关结构的变化；(3) 最差标准化AR(1)创新（temporal）捕捉时序自回归残差中的异常。三通道各自得到秩后经Fisher方法聚合，再将聚合量与此前历史聚合量的秩进行比较，从而输出窗口级异常排名。评估设定为：2019年1–4月拟合、2019年7–12月作为开发证据、2020年数据在检查前冻结作为留出集，并公开披露通道级消融结果。

Result: 在六条英国电网数据流上，TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah在2019年测试窗口中排为第一，但公开的通道消融显示该最高排名由局部（local）通道贡献，而不是copula-form通道——后者单独将Atiyah排在第59位。2019年8月9日的短暂频率事件被融合检测器排在143位，远低于纯时序通道单独给出的40位，而重构类基线将其列为首位。2020年冻结集无窗口被选中，作者解释为记录规则饱和（record-rule saturation）所致而非当年太平无事；冻结集中排名最高的窗口事后被解读为Storm Ellen。

Conclusion: 全文贯穿三项解释性限制：所得p值是选择/排序量，不能理解为事件概率；copula-form通道并非字面上的copula密度（方法未做概率积分或正态分数变换）；经验秩计数仅是诊断性证据，不能当作覆盖率或假发现率的证明。同时强调论文中所有图表均由图灵完备的可复现机器可读报告生成，以支撑其可审计性主张。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [290] [ConceptTS: LLM-Guided Concept Bottlenecks for Interpretable Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21277)
*Yichen Jiang,Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTS 是一个可解释的多变量时间序列预测框架：它利用大语言模型（LLM）提出带有名称、人类可读的概念并自动生成可执行的标注规则，避免昂贵的人工概念标注；概念被组织为三个互补的瓶颈（历史背景、局部预测区间、完整预测范围），共享解码器基于概念激活表示构建预测，从而让决策过程显式化并支持概念层面的干预。在北京市多站点空气质量数据集上，ConceptTS 在精度上与强黑盒基线相当，同时产生语义上有意义的概念激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多元时间序列预测器虽能建模复杂的时序与跨变量依赖，但其表示不透明，难以解释特定预测为何产生；缺乏透明度限制了它们在要求从业者理解并评估预测影响因素场景中的应用。作者旨在在保持高精度的同时提供可解释性。

Method: CoreNTTS 框架包含三部分：(1) 用 LLM 提出任务相关概念并生成可执行的标注规则，将语言模型的领域知识转化为直接监督，无需人工概念标注；(2) 将概念组织为三个互补瓶颈，分别刻画历史上下文、局部预测区间与完整预测范围；(3) 一个共享解码器结合各瓶颈预测激活得到的表示来构造预测，使决策过程显式化并支持概念级干预。

Result: 在北京市多站点空气质量数据集上的实验表明，ConceptTS 达到了与强黑盒基线相当甚至具有竞争力的预测精度，同时生成了语义上有意义的概念激活。

Conclusion: ConceptTS 证明了可解释机制（基于 LLM 自动生成的概念与瓶颈结构）能够在不过度牺牲预测性能的情况下提供透明、支持干预的多元时间序列预测，为高风险场景中的可信预测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multivariate time-series forecasters can model complex temporal and cross-variable dependencies, yet their opaque representations provide limited insight into why a particular forecast is produced. This lack of transparency restricts their use in settings where practitioners must understand and assess the factors underlying a prediction. We introduce ConceptTS, an interpretable forecasting framework that organizes its predictions around named, human-readable concepts. ConceptTS uses a large language model to propose task-relevant concepts and generate executable labeling rules, translating the language model's domain knowledge into direct supervision without costly manual concept annotation. The proposed concepts are organized into three complementary bottlenecks that describe the historical context, local forecast intervals, and the full forecast horizon. A shared decoder combines representations derived from their predicted activations to construct the forecast, making the model's decision process explicit and supporting direct concept-level interventions. Experiments on the Beijing Multi-Site Air Quality dataset show that ConceptTS achieves accuracy competitive with strong black-box baselines while producing semantically meaningful concept activations.

</details>


### [291] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SPARCL，一种光谱分区解析持续学习器，通过将运行自相关矩阵分解为高能量核心与残差补集、冻结旧类分类器核心子空间分量、仅用递归最小二乘更新残差块，从而解决解析类增量学习中的“光谱干扰”导致的旧类漂移问题；在冻结 ViT-B/16 协议下于 CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R 和 ImageNet-A 上大幅缩小了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的解析持续学习方法虽用闭式岭回归替代迭代优化，避免梯度覆盖式遗忘，但即使在精确递归求解下旧类仍会漂移。以往基于随机梯度覆盖的遗忘叙事无法解释该现象。作者识别出真正的根源为“光谱干扰”：所有任务共享联合岭分类器的逆自相关算子 $(R+\lambda I)^{-1}$，新任务样本加载到旧主导特征方向上时会稀释谱并扰动旧类 logits，即便旧标签不再被访问。

Method: SPARCL 将运行自相关矩阵分解为高能量核心子空间与残差补集；冻结核心子空间中旧类分类器分量以提供不变性保证，仅通过递归最小二乘（可选伴随残差随机投影扩展）更新残差块。整体构成一个简单闭式更新，且对旧 logits 的核心贡献具有可证明的不变性保证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R 和 ImageNet-A 数据集上、采用冻结 ViT-B/16 协议时，SPARCL 缩小了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法之间的大部分性能差距，并与稀疏特征去相关方法（如 Fly-CL）互补。

Conclusion: 光谱干扰（而非随机梯度覆盖）才是解析持续学习方法旧类漂移的成因；通过在谱域上将模型划分为冻结核心与可更新残差，可实现不依赖样本回放的闭式解，兼顾稳定性（旧类）与可塑性（新类），并为解析持续学习提供了新的理论视角和可证明保证的实用算法。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [292] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 E²-TTT（Expressive and Efficient TTT），在保持逐 token 更新时序结构的前提下，推导出可精确复现 chunk 末尾 fast-weight 与动量状态的闭式状态转移公式，从而在 chunk 级实现完全并行化训练，同时兼顾逐 token 更新的表达力与 chunk 级近似的硬件效率。在 1.3B 参数规模上验证，该方法的语言建模性能与现有 TTT 及混合注意力基线相当，在上下文检索上更优，在长度外推（Needle in a Haystack 测试中训练长度的 8 倍下仍保持 90%+ 准确率）上优势最为显著，且训练吞吐与高效 chunk 级方法持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT 通过在推理期间持续更新权重实现长上下文处理，但现有方法难以兼顾逐 token 更新动态的表达力与基于 chunk 近似的高硬件效率——前者保留完整时序信息却难以并行，后者可并行却丢弃了更新规则的时序结构。

Method: 在"梯度在 chunk 起始权重处计算"这一标准近似下，推导出一个闭式状态转移公式，能够精确再现逐 token 循环在 chunk 末尾的 fast-weight 与动量状态。由此实现 chunk 级训练的完全并行化，同时不丢失逐 token 更新规则的时序结构（该结构正是此前 chunk 级方法所丢弃的）。

Result: 从零训练至 1.3B 参数规模的模型，语言建模性能与先前 TTT 及混合注意力基线相当，在上下文内检索任务上优于这些基线；在长度外推上优势最突出——标准 Needle in a Haystack passkey 测试中，在训练上下文长度的 8 倍处仍保持超过 90% 的准确率；训练吞吐可与高效 chunk 级方法匹配。

Conclusion: E²-TTT 有效调和了表达力与效率之间的矛盾：既能保持 chunk 级训练的高吞吐与并行性，又能保留逐 token 更新时序带来的外推与检索能力，为长上下文 TTT 模型提供了可行的设计方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [293] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间感知Transformer的AECOPD（慢性阻塞性肺疾病急性加重）预测模型，利用日常家用呼吸机产生的呼吸数据，在家庭监护场景下实现对急性加重的及时预测，并在多个分类任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD症状变化迅速，迫切需要具有时间敏感性的预测模型。然而，现有大多数针对AECOPD的机器学习模型依赖临床和实验室数据，这不可避免地会造成预测延迟。为了确保AECOPD的及时检测并最大程度减少延迟，本文聚焦于仅能获取家用呼吸机呼吸数据的家庭监护场景。

Method: 引入一种时间感知（Time-Aware）Transformer的AECOPD预测模型。该模型利用时间感知Transformer从呼吸机数据中捕获症状及其时间进展，生成有意义的患者表示，从而支持预测任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该时间感知Transformer方法在多个分类任务中均优于传统方法，展现出提升AECOPD预测准确性的潜力。

Conclusion: 时间感知Transformer在家庭监护场景下利用呼吸数据预测AECOPD具有明显优势，能够减少传统临床数据带来的延迟，有望提升AECOPD预测的准确性与及时性。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [294] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究短观测期定价面板中"观测数量多但价格变动少"这一稀疏定价特征对统计推断的影响。作者将不确定性与"给定已实现价格轨迹下的估计误差"和"同一生成过程产生的不同轨迹之间的变化"分离，发现基线模拟中后者占梯度提升模型估计误差方差的97.6%，且面板内重抽样无法识别这一设计间成分。文章给出三个结果：设计间离散度符合经验关系σ≈0.182V^(-0.271)（V=价格变动次数×变动幅度平方）；共享价格路径的区域不能创造独立轨迹，而独立设计误差的单位平均以标准平方根速率降低离散度；跨独立定价单位估计的Paule-Mandel方差成分将模拟覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。核心启示是应转向设计能产生独立识别变异的数据生成过程，而不是依赖固定被动面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多短期观测定价面板虽包含大量观测，但仅有少量不同的价格变动，这使得基于单个观测面板的传统推断方法可能系统性低估估计误差。作者旨在从推断角度阐明稀疏定价机制的影响，并指出仅依赖一个已实现价格轨迹会忽略由同一生成过程不同轨迹引起的设计间不确定性，从而为研究设计提供新的导向。

Method: 作者使用以稀疏定价机制标定的合成数据生成过程进行模拟，将估计误差方差分解为：轨迹内（conditional on a realised price trajectory）不确定性与轨迹间（across alternative trajectories）变异两部分。通过蒙特卡洛模拟估计跨设计的离散度，并提出σ≈0.182V^(-0.271)的经验关系（V=价格变动次数×幅度平方）。通过比较共享价格路径的区域与具有独立设计误差的单位（平方根速率去噪），以及跨独立定价单位估计Paule-Mandel方差成分来检验推断改进效果。

Result: 基线模拟中，跨轨迹设计成分占估计误差方差的97.6%，面板内重抽样无法识别该成分。设计间离散度符合σ≈0.182V^(-0.271)；共享价格路径的区域只降低结果噪声、不产生独立轨迹；独立单位平均可使离散度按平方根速率下降；跨单位Paule-Mandel方差成分将同质模拟的经验覆盖率从0.469提高到0.931。

Conclusion: 在稀疏定价面板中，估计误差方差主要由设计间（跨轨迹）成分主导，而基于单一已实现轨迹的推断手段无法捕获这一不确定性。研究启示是应构建能创造独立识别性变异的多种数据生成过程/实验设计，而非依赖单一固定被动面板，以实现更可靠的不确定性与覆盖度量。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [295] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对自我精炼（self-refinement）流水线中的生成、批判、修订三个阶段进行了模型规模（模型大小）的逐阶段研究，基于5个跨领域基准、Qwen3的6种规模和Gemma 3的4种规模得出结论：更大的生成器与修订器通常提升整体性能（而过小的修订器反而有害）；批判器的规模对性能高度不敏感，但即使很小的批判器也优于完全省略批判环节。研究表明模型容量不应在三个阶段中均匀分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我精炼（生成-批判-修订）是提升LLM生成质量的广泛范式，也是许多LLM智能体的核心机制。三个阶段具有不同的认知需求，但现有方法大多把模型规模当作实现细节而非研究对象，可能导致计算资源浪费，且缺乏关于模型规模如何影响各阶段、以及有效自我精炼是否需要三个阶段同等能力模型的系统性研究。

Method: 采用逐阶段模型规模研究方法：在5个来自不同领域的基准上，使用Qwen3的6种模型规模和Gemma 3的4种模型规模，对自我精炼流水线的生成器、批判器、修订器分别进行规模变体实验，系统比较各阶段规模变化对整体流水线性能的影响。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 更大的生成器和修订器一般能提升流水线整体性能，而规模不足的修订器甚至可能损害性能；(2) 性能对批判器的规模高度不敏感，但纳入即使很小的批判器也始终优于完全省略批判环节；(3) 模型容量不应在自我精炼流水线中均匀分配，不同阶段具有不同的规模缩放特性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自我精炼各阶段对模型规模的不同敏感度，否定了"三个环节需要同等能力模型"的隐含假设，为设计计算效率更高的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实用指导——应优先扩容生成器与修订器，批判环节可用小模型完成。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [When Generated Images Look Right and Retrieve Wrong: Coverage-Guided Cross-Scale Re-Indexing for Knowledge-Faithful Generative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20810)
*Guangyuan Dong,Chuang Liu,Yangchen Zeng,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyang Yu,Pinlong Zhao,Yuchao Hou,Ziwei Li,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出CERES，一种闭环多模态索引框架，通过三级语义金字塔、共现感知路由器、尺度路由交叉注意力以及基于冻结视觉语言模型（VLM）的再索引验证，解决多尺度场景下语言引导生成器因使用全局池化文本嵌入而丢失尺度特定概念、破坏概念-查询检索的"语义坍缩"问题，并在全色锐化及检索指标上取得新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态信息系统常将生成的视觉内容重新路由回生成时所用的同一视觉-语言索引，因此生成结果必须能被其服务的查询检索到。但当场景包含尺度差异极大的实体时，现有语言引导生成器仅以单个全局池化文本嵌入为条件，会静默丢弃尺度特定概念，即使像素保真度高也会破坏概念-查询检索。作者将这一失效形式化为"语义坍缩"（semantic collapse），并希望构建一个能保持查询内容可检索的闭环生成框架。

Method: 提出CERES闭环多模态索引框架：1）构建三级语义金字塔；2）通过共现感知路由器挖掘隐式概念；3）在轻量U-Net生成器中执行尺度路由的交叉注意力；4）用同一冻结VLM对生成图像重新索引以验证概念覆盖率。训练采用连续可微的soft-Jaccard覆盖率目标，在显式非退化条件下为仅0.39M参数的生成器提供稠密梯度；覆盖率另由仅基于外部场景与物体标签训练的独立DINOv2线性探针验证。

Result: 在四个全色锐化基准、七种设置下取得新SOTA，且在尺度变化最剧烈的场景中增益最大。相比最强基线，概念-查询检索Recall@5提升+14.0个百分点，图像-文本平均倒数排名（MRR）提升0.19，表明闭环保留的是可查询内容而非自指的（self-referential）特征一致性。

Conclusion: 闭环的多模态索引框架通过明确的尺度感知条件注入与再索引覆盖率验证，能有效消除语义坍缩，兼顾像素质量与查询可检索性，验证了"以检索闭环约束生成"这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal information systems increasingly route generated visual content back through the same vision-language index that informed its production, so the output must remain retrievable by the queries it was meant to serve. When the scene contains entities at vastly different scales, existing language-guided generators condition on a single, globally pooled text embedding and quietly drop scale-specific concepts, breaking concept-query retrieval even when pixel fidelity is high. We formalise this failure as semantic collapse and propose CERES, a closed-loop multimodal indexing framework that builds a three-level semantic pyramid, mines implicit concepts via a co-occurrence-aware router, performs scale-routed cross-attention into a lightweight U-Net generator, and verifies coverage by re-indexing the generated image with the same frozen VLM. A continuously differentiable soft-Jaccard coverage objective returns dense gradients to the 0.39M-parameter generator under explicit non-degeneracy conditions, and coverage is verified by an independent DINOv2 linear probe trained only on external scene and object labels. On four pansharpening benchmarks across seven settings, CERES delivers the new state of the art with the largest gains where scale variation is most extreme. It also improves concept-query retrieval Recall@5 by +14.0 points and image-text mean reciprocal rank by 0.19 over the strongest baseline, showing that the closed loop preserves queryable content rather than self-referential feature consistency.

</details>
